.[MobspSearchCompanyQuickI]
(		
/* 
	EXEC MobspSearchCompanyQuickI 
	@Val = N'ایزی', 
	@UserID = 3,
	@LanguageID  = 1065,
	@DefaultLanguageID  = 1065
*/
@Val NVARCHAR(200),
@UserID INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
DECLARE @fld VARCHAR(MAX) = '', @fldLimit VARCHAR(MAX) = '', @from VARCHAR(2000) = '', @term VARCHAR(MAX) = '', @FinalQry NVARCHAR(MAX) = ''
/**/
EXEC MobspSetcmpDataPermission @UserID
DECLARE @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(4000)
SELECT 
	@cmpDataPermision = TXT
FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 20
AND UserId = @UserId
/**/
IF(ISNULL(@Val,'') <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @Val = REPLACE(@Val,' ','%')
END
/**/
DECLARE @ReplacedCharVal NVARCHAR(MAX)
SET @ReplacedCharVal = REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
		 NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))

/*--*/
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SET @fld = 		
		' SELECT DISTINCT TOP 100
		[CompanyID]
		,CP.[CompanyPersonID]
		,P.[ProjectID]
		,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]) + '' '' ,'''') +
						ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
				ELSE (SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId) + '' '' ,'''')  +
							ISNULL(prsFName + '' '' , '''') + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
						FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
						WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
						)				
		END) [CompanyName]
		,(ISNULL((SELECT CPT.PersonTitle FROM dbo.CRMPersonTitle CPT WHERE CPT.PersonTitleID = CP.prsTitleId) + '' '' ,'''') +
					ISNULL(CP.prsFName + '' '', '''') +
					ISNULL(CP.prsLName, '''')
		) [CompanyPerson]
		,P.[prjTitle] + ISNULL('' '' + P.[prjPostFix], '''') [ProjectTitle]
		,ISNULL( 
			ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId) + '' '', '''') + 
				ISNULL(cmpName, '''') +  
			ISNULL('' - '' + (
				ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle WHERE PersontitleId = CP.prsTitleId) + '' '', '''') +
			ISNULL(prsFName + '' '', '''') + ISNULL(prsLName, '''') ),'''')			
			+ '' - '' + (P.[prjTitle] + ISNULL('' '' + P.[prjPostFix], ''''))
		,'''') [FullText]
		,[cmpNumber]
		,[cmpAddress]
		,[cmpCountryId]
		,[cmpProvinceId]
		,[cmpCityId]
		,[cmpCoordinateLatitude]
		,[cmpCoordinateLongitude]
		,[cmpCoordinateZoomLevel]
		,[cmpGroupIdII]
		'
	/*--------------------------------*/
	SET @fldLimit = 
			' SELECT DISTINCT TOP 100
			[CompanyID]
			,CP.[CompanyPersonID]
			,P.[ProjectID]
			,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
					THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]) + '' '','''') + 
							ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
					ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId) + '' '','''') +
								ISNULL(prsFName + '' '' ,'''') + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
							FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
							WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
							)				
			END) [CompanyName]
			,(ISNULL((SELECT CPT.PersonTitle FROM dbo.CRMPersonTitle CPT WHERE CPT.PersonTitleID = CP.prsTitleId) + '' '' ,'''') +
					ISNULL(CP.prsFName + '' '', '''') +
					ISNULL(CP.prsLName, '''')
			) [CompanyPerson]
			,P.[prjTitle] + ISNULL('' '' + P.[prjPostFix], '''') [ProjectTitle]
			,ISNULL(
				ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId) + '' '', '''') + 
					ISNULL(cmpName, '''')  +  	
				ISNULL('' - '' + (
					ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle WHERE PersontitleId = CP.prsTitleId) + '' '', '''') +
				ISNULL(prsFName + '' '', '''') + ISNULL(prsLName, '''')),'''')  + 
				+ '' - '' + (P.[prjTitle] + ISNULL('' '' + P.[prjPostFix], ''''))
			,'''') [FullText]
			,[cmpNumber]
			,[cmpAddress]
			,[cmpCountryId]
			,[cmpProvinceId]
			,[cmpCityId]
			,[cmpCoordinateLatitude]
			,[cmpCoordinateLongitude]
			,[cmpCoordinateZoomLevel]
			,[cmpGroupIdII]
			'
	/*--------------------------------*/					
	SET @term = ' WHERE (
	(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(REPLACE(cmpName, '' '' ,'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
	 NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' + @ReplacedCharVal +'%'')'
	+ ' OR (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(REPLACE(CP.prsLName, '' '' ,'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' + 
		@ReplacedCharVal +'%'')'

	SET @term += ' OR C.cmpNumber Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.cmpPreTel Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.cmpPreTelII Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+   
				' OR C.cmpTel Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.cmpTelII Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.cmpFax Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.cmpMobile Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(C.cmpaddress AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' + 
				@ReplacedCharVal +'%'''+
				' OR  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(REPLACE(CP.prsFName, '' '' ,'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' + 
				@ReplacedCharVal  +'%'''+	    
				' OR CP.prsTel Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR CP.prsTelII Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR CP.prsMobile Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR CP.prsFax Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.CmpCode Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.cmpNationalCode Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(REPLACE(C.cmpNameEng, '' '' ,'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' + 
				@ReplacedCharVal  +'%'''+	       
				' OR CP.prsNationalCode Like ''%' + @Val  +'%''' + 
				')'
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SET @fld = 
		' SELECT DISTINCT TOP 100
		[CompanyID]
		,CP.[CompanyPersonID]
		,P.[ProjectID]
		,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '),'''') + '' '' + 
						ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '),'''')))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '),'''')  + '' '' +
							ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '),'''')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END) [CompanyName]
		,(SELECT 
			ISNULL((SELECT CPT.PersonTitle FROM dbo.CRMPersonTitle_Lang CPT WHERE CPT.PersonTitleID = CP.prsTitleId AND CPT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '),'''') + '' '' +
			ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CPLN.prsLName, '''') FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '),'''')
		) [CompanyPerson]
		,(SELECT [prjTitle] FROM dbo.CrmProjects_Lang PL WHERE PL.ProjectID = P.[ProjectID] AND PL.LanguageId = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ')
			+ ISNULL('' '' + (SELECT [prjPostFix] FROM dbo.CrmProjects_Lang PL WHERE PL.ProjectID = P.[ProjectID] AND PL.LanguageId = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''') [ProjectTitle]
		,ISNULL(
			ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle_Lang CTLN WHERE CTLN.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId  AND CTLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''') + 
			'' ''+ ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''')  + 
			ISNULL('' - '' + (
			ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle_Lang PTLN WHERE PTLN.PersontitleId = CP.prsTitleId  AND PTLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''')+ '' '' +
			ISNULL((SELECT prsFName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''') 
			+ '' ''+ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), ''''))  ,'''')		
		,'''') [FullText] 
		,[cmpNumber]
		,(SELECT [cmpAddress] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CCL WHERE CCL.[CompanyID] = C.CompanyID AND CCL.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ') [cmpAddress]
		,[cmpCountryId]
		,[cmpProvinceId]
		,[cmpCityId]
		,[cmpCoordinateLatitude]
		,[cmpCoordinateLongitude]
		,[cmpCoordinateZoomLevel]
		,[cmpGroupIdII]
		'
	/*--------------------------------*/
	SET @fldLimit = 
			' SELECT DISTINCT TOP 100
			[CompanyID]
			,CP.[CompanyPersonID]
			,P.[ProjectID]
			,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
					THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '),'''') + '' '' + 
							ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '),'''')))
					ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '),'''')  + '' '' +
								ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '),'''')
						FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
						WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
						)				
				END) [CompanyName]
			,(SELECT 
				ISNULL((SELECT CPT.PersonTitle FROM dbo.CRMPersonTitle_Lang CPT WHERE CPT.PersonTitleID = CP.prsTitleId AND CPT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '),'''') + '' '' +
				ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CPLN.prsLName, '''') FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '),'''')
			) [CompanyPerson]
			,(SELECT [prjTitle] FROM dbo.CrmProjects_Lang PL WHERE PL.ProjectID = P.[ProjectID] AND PL.LanguageId = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ')
				+ ISNULL('' '' + (SELECT [prjPostFix] FROM dbo.CrmProjects_Lang PL WHERE PL.ProjectID = P.[ProjectID] AND PL.LanguageId = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''') [ProjectTitle]
			,ISNULL(
				ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle_Lang CTLN WHERE CTLN.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId AND CTLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''') + 
				'' ''+ ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''')  +
				ISNULL('' - '' + 		
				(ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle_Lang PTLN WHERE PTLN.PersontitleId = CP.prsTitleId AND PTLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''')+ '' '' +
				ISNULL((SELECT prsFName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''') + '' ''
				+ ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''')),'''')   			
			,'''') [FullText]
			,[cmpNumber]
			,(SELECT [cmpAddress] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CCL WHERE CCL.[CompanyID] = C.CompanyID AND CCL.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ') [cmpAddress]
			,[cmpCountryId]
			,[cmpProvinceId]
			,[cmpCityId]
			,[cmpCoordinateLatitude]
			,[cmpCoordinateLongitude]
			,[cmpCoordinateZoomLevel]
			,[cmpGroupIdII]
			'
	/*--------------------------------*/							
	SET @term = ' WHERE (
	(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT REPLACE(cmpName, '' '' ,'''') FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), cmpName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
	 NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' + 
		@ReplacedCharVal +'%'')'
	+ ' OR (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' 
	+ CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), CP.prsLName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' + 
		@ReplacedCharVal +'%'')'

	
	SET @term += ' OR C.cmpNumber Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.cmpPreTel Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.cmpPreTelII Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+   
				' OR C.cmpTel Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.cmpTelII Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.cmpFax Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.cmpMobile Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR  ISNULL((SELECT REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(cmpaddress AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CCLN WHERE CCLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CCLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''') Like ''%' + 
					@ReplacedCharVal +'%'''+	       
				' OR  ISNULL((SELECT REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(REPLACE(prsFName, '' '' ,'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang PPLN WHERE PPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID AND PPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''') Like ''%' + 
				@ReplacedCharVal  +'%'''+	  
				' OR CP.prsTel Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR CP.prsTelII Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR CP.prsMobile Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR CP.prsFax Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.CmpCode Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR C.cmpNationalCode Like ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				' OR  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(REPLACE(C.cmpNameEng, '' '' ,'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' + 
				@ReplacedCharVal  +'%'''+	     
				' OR CP.prsNationalCode Like ''%' + @Val  +'%''' +
				')'
END		
/**/
SET @from = ' From dbo.CrmCompany C 
				LEFT JOIN 
					dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP 
						ON (C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId  
							AND	(CP.[FirstRegisterUserId] IN 
								(SELECT ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' UNION 
								SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers 
								WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 23)
								OR (CP.[FirstRegisterUserId] IS NULL)
								) 
							)
				LEFT JOIN [dbo].[CrmProjects] P
					ON (C.CompanyID = P.[prjCompanyId]
						AND	(P.[prjStarterUserId] IN 
							(SELECT ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' UNION 
							SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 24)
							OR (P.[prjStarterUserId] IS NULL)
							)
						AND P.[prjTitle] NOT IN 
							(SELECT ForbiddenProjectTitle 
							FROM CrmProjectsForbiddenForUser
							WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + '
							) 
						)
			'

/**/
IF @cmpDataPermision <> ''
BEGIN
	SET @FinalQry = 'SELECT * FROM (' +
		' SELECT * FROM ( ' +
			@fld + @from + @term + @cmpDataPermision +
		' ) C union ' + 
		' SELECT * FROM ( ' +
			@fldLimit + @from + @term +
		') d 
		) tmp
		WHERE CompanyID IN ( SELECT CompanyId '+
			@from + @term + @cmpDataPermision
		+' )'
END
ELSE
BEGIN
	SET @FinalQry = @fld + @from + @term
END

EXECUTE(@FinalQry)

END







GO
-- [dbo].[MobspSearchCompanyWithName] | References permission/temp-filter machinery






/*ESP951002951103951104*/
/*RZ960411- Change othersFields to otherFields & Add otherFieldsHeader & Add multi language & Edit marketer & Edit cmpSpecialPoint*/
/*RZ960422- Add Tag OrderView To otherFields & otherFieldsHeader & Add user permission*/
/*RZ960431- Change condition for search in persian*/
/*RZ960502- Add 021 when perTel is null*/
/*RZ960602- Change cmpFristRegisterUserId to FristRegisterUserId And Importdate to FristRegisterDate*/
/*RZ960604- Change | ,^ to @|$|@ , @^$|@*/
/*RZ960607- Change sp MobspSelectCompanyListWithName to MobspSearchCompanyWithName And change 1610 to 1740 ,...*/
/*RZ960612- Change 1740 to 1610 ,...*/
/*RZ960912- Add pagation*/
/*RZ961103- Add search prsLName*/
/*RZ961205- Change all*/
/*RZ970215- Add  Total Count*/
/*AI990825- remove all ISNULL() with -1 AND ''*/
/*AI990826- check if company has logo by returning the logo id*/
/*AI14000710 - Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
/*AI14010608 - Replace space to % in search value - Change condition - change Name length from 100 to 200*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspSearchCompanyWithName]
(	
/* 
	EXEC [MobspSearchCompanyWithName] 3, N'a' , 1033, 1065,N'', 1, 45
*/
/*RZ960422- Add user permission*/
@UserId INT,
@Name NVARCHAR(200), /*AI14010608*/
/*RZ960411 For multi language*/
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
@PersonLabel NVARCHAR(MAX),
/*RZ960912- Add pagation*/
@PageNumber INT = 1,
@PageSize INT = 10
)
AS 
BEGIN
/**/
/*RZ961205- Change all*/
/*RZ960422- Add user permission*/
EXEC dbo.MobspSetcmpDataPermission @UserID
DECLARE @clmn VARCHAR(max), @FROM VARCHAR(max), @Term VARCHAR(max)

/*AI14010608- Replace space to % in search value*/
IF(ISNULL(@Name,'') <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @Name = REPLACE(@Name,' ','%')
END
/*AI14010608- End*/

IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	Set @clmn = 
		'SELECT DISTINCT
		C.CompanyID
		,C.[cmpNumber]
		/*,C.[cmpName] AI14000710*/
		,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')
			ELSE (SELECT ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]			
				)				
			END) [cmpName] /*AI14000710*/
		,C.[cmpTitleId]
		,C.[cmpPreTel]
		,C.[cmpTel]
		,C.[cmpTelUpTo]
		,C.[cmpTelDesc]
		,C.[cmpPreTelII]
		,C.[cmpTelII]
		,C.[cmpTelUpToII]
		,C.[cmpTelIIDesc]
		,C.[cmpProvinceId]				      
		,C.[cmpCityId]
		,C.[cmpReferUserId]
		,C.[FirstRegisterUserId]
		,C.[FirstRegisterDate]
		,C.[cmpCoordinateLatitude]
		,C.[cmpCoordinateLongitude]
		,(SELECT dbo.fnFormatPersianDate(C.[FirstRegisterDatePersian],''/'')) [FirstRegisterDatePersian]
		/*AI990826*/
		,(SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyLoGoID] FROM CrmCompanyLoGo CCL WHERE CCL.CompanyId = C.CompanyID ORDER BY CCL.RegisterDate DESC) [cmpLogoId]
		/**/							
		/*,CASE C.cmpName 
		WHEN ''' + @PersonLabel +''' THEN 
			ISNULL([cmpName],'''') + 
			'' ('' + ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 
						ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle prsT WHERE prsT.PersonTitleID = prs.prsTitleId) + '' '' , '''') +
						ISNULL(prs.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prs.prsLName, '''')
					FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons prs WHERE prs.prsCompanyID = C.CompanyID
					ORDER BY ISNULL(prs.prsOrderView, prs.CompanyPersonID)),'''') + '') ''								        
		ELSE 
			ISNULL((SELECT CT.CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId),'''') + '' '' +
			ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')
		END [CompanyName] AI14000710	*/

			LEFT JOIN 
				dbo.CrmCompanyPersons P 
					ON (C.CompanyID = P.prsCompanyId  
						AND	(P.[FirstRegisterUserId] IN 
							(SELECT ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' UNION 
							SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers 
							WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 23)
							OR (P.[FirstRegisterUserId] IS NULL)
							) 
						) WHERE 1=1'

DECLARE @Mainqry VARCHAR(MAX)
SET @Mainqry = ';WITH CtePaging  AS (' + 
				'SELECT 
				CAST(COUNT(*) OVER() AS BIGINT) AS TotalCount, /*RZ970215- Add  Total Count*/
				Row_number() OVER(ORDER BY CompanyId DESC) AS RowNum,
				CASE  WHEN (COUNT(*) OVER() - ((COUNT(*) OVER() / ' + CAST(@PageSize AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ') * ' + CAST(@PageSize AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ') ) = 0
					THEN
						COUNT(*) OVER() / ' + CAST(@PageSize AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '
					ELSE
						(COUNT(*) OVER() / ' + CAST(@PageSize AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ') + 1
					END
				AS AllPage,							
				*				
				FROM(' + 
					@clmn + @FROM + @Term + @cmpDataPermision /*+ ' ORDER BY CompanyId DESC'*/	
				+') AS temTbl' 													
				+') 
				SELECT 
					* 	
				FROM   CtePaging 
				WHERE  RowNum BETWEEN ( ' + CAST(@PageNumber AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '- 1 ) * ' + CAST(@PageSize AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ' + 1 AND ' + CAST(@PageNumber AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ' * ' + CAST(@PageSize AS VARCHAR(MAX)) 

EXECUTE (@Mainqry)
/*=============================== Paging END =================================*/
/*--*/
DELETE dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 10
AND UserId = @UserId
/*--*/
INSERT INTO dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo (
	UserId,	TXT, TypeId)
 VALUES ( @UserId, ' SELECT CompanyId ' + @from + @Term, 10 ) 
/*RZ960422- Add user permission*/

END

/*RZ961025*/
/*RZ970215- add top from 100 to 500*/
/*AI981212 added CompanyGroupIIRate , cmpName and removed cmpId */
/*AI981213 AI added cmpAddress*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspSearchQuickCompany]
(		
/* 
	EXEC MobspSearchQuickCompany
	@Val = N'رایان نور', 
	@UserID = 3,
	@LanguageID  = 1065,
	@DefaultLanguageID  = 1065
		
*/
@Val NVARCHAR(50), 
@UserID INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)

AS
BEGIN
DECLARE @fld VARCHAR(MAX), @fldLimit VARCHAR(MAX), @from VARCHAR(2000), @term VARCHAR(MAX)
/*--*/
EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @UserID
DECLARE @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(4000)
SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT
FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 20
AND UserId = @UserId
/*--*/
/*--*/
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	/*RZ970215- add top from 100 to 500*/
	SET @fld = 		
		' SELECT DISTINCT TOP 500 CompanyID, ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId), '''') + 
		'' ''+ ISNULL(cmpName, '''') cmpName,
		ISNULL((SELECT CG.Rate FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroupsII CG WHERE CG.CompanyGroupIIID = C.cmpGroupIdII) , 0) CompanyGroupIIRate,
		ISNULL(cmpAddress, '''') cmpAddress,
		ISNULL( 
		ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId), '''') + 
		'' ''+ ISNULL(cmpName, '''') +  
		ISNULL('' - '' +(
		ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle WHERE PersontitleId = CP.prsTitleId), '''')+ '' '' +
		ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' ''+ISNULL(prsLName, '''') ),'''')			
		+ ISNULL( '' - '' + CAST(C.CmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(20)),'''')		
		,'''') CompanyPerson '
	/*--------------------------------*/
	/*RZ970215- add top from 100 to 500*/
	SET @fldLimit = 
			' SELECT DISTINCT TOP 500 CompanyID, ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId), '''') + 
			'' ''+ ISNULL(cmpName, '''') cmpName,
			ISNULL((SELECT CG.Rate FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroupsII CG WHERE CG.CompanyGroupIIID = C.cmpGroupIdII) , 0) CompanyGroupIIRate,
			ISNULL(cmpAddress, '''') cmpAddress,
			ISNULL(
			ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId), '''') + 
			'' ''+ ISNULL(cmpName, '''')  +  	
			ISNULL('' - '' + (
			ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle WHERE PersontitleId = CP.prsTitleId), '''')+ '' '' +
			ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' ''+ISNULL(prsLName, '''')),'''')  + 
			ISNULL('' - '' +CAST(C.CmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(20)),'''')			
			,'''') CompanyPerson '
	/*--------------------------------*/							
	SET @term = ' WHERE (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(cmpName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
	 NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' + 
		REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
		 NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'')'
	+ ' OR (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(CP.prsLName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' + 
		REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'')'	
END
ELSE IF (@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	/*RZ970215- add top from 100 to 500*/
	SET @fld = 
		' SELECT DISTINCT TOP 500 CompanyID, 
		ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle_Lang CTLN WHERE CTLN.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId  AND CTLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''') + 
		'' ''+ ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''') cmpName,
		ISNULL((SELECT CG.Rate FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroupsII CG WHERE CG.CompanyGroupIIID = C.cmpGroupIdII) , 0) CompanyGroupIIRate,
		ISNULL((SELECT cmpAddress FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''') cmpAddress,
		ISNULL(
		ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle_Lang CTLN WHERE CTLN.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId  AND CTLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''') + 
		'' ''+ ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''')  + 
		ISNULL('' - '' + (
		ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle_Lang PTLN WHERE PTLN.PersontitleId = CP.prsTitleId  AND PTLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''')+ '' '' +
		ISNULL((SELECT prsFName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''') 
		+ '' ''+ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), ''''))  ,'''')		
		+ ISNULL( '' - '' +  CAST(C.CmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(20)),''''),'''') CompanyPerson '
	/*--------------------------------*/
	/*RZ970215- add top from 100 to 500*/
	SET @fldLimit = 
			' SELECT DISTINCT TOP 500 CompanyID, 
			ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle_Lang CTLN WHERE CTLN.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId AND CTLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''') + 
			'' ''+ ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''') cmpName,
			ISNULL((SELECT CG.Rate FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroupsII CG WHERE CG.CompanyGroupIIID = C.cmpGroupIdII) , 0) CompanyGroupIIRate,
			ISNULL((SELECT cmpAddress FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''') cmpAddress,
			ISNULL(
			ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle_Lang CTLN WHERE CTLN.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId AND CTLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''') + 
			'' ''+ ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''')  +
			ISNULL('' - '' + 		
			(ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle_Lang PTLN WHERE PTLN.PersontitleId = CP.prsTitleId AND PTLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''')+ '' '' +
			ISNULL((SELECT prsFName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''') + '' ''
			+ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), '''')),'''')   			
			+ ISNULL('' - '' +  CAST(C.CmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(20)),'''')			
			,'''') CompanyPerson '
	/*--------------------------------*/							
	SET @term = ' WHERE (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), cmpName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
	 NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' + 
		REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
		 NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'')'
	+ ' OR (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' 
	+ CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '), CP.prsLName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' + 
		REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'')'									
END		
SET @from = ' FROM dbo.CRMCompany C LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId'		
/*--*/
/*--*/
/*--*/
IF @cmpDataPermision <> ''
BEGIN
EXECUTE(
' SELECT * FROM ( ' +
@fld+ @from+ @term +@cmpDataPermision +
' ) C union ' + 
' SELECT * FROM ( ' +
@fldLimit+ @from+ @term +
') d
where CompanyId Not IN ( SELECT CompanyId '+
@from+ @term +@cmpDataPermision +' )'
)
END
ELSE
EXECUTE(@fld+ @from+ @term)

END

@UserId INT,

@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
AS 
BEGIN
/**/

DECLARE @clmn VARCHAR(MAX), @FROM VARCHAR(MAX), @Term VARCHAR(MAX)

BEGIN
	SET @clmn = 
		'SELECT DISTINCT
		C.CompanyID

			THEN ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')

				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]			
				)

		,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
					ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
						ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]	
						
				)				
			END) [CompanyName]						
		,(SELECT ISNULL(FName , '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(LName,'''') FROM dbo.Users U WHERE U.UserId = C.cmpReferUserId) [cmpReferUser]						
		'
/*--*/

	SET @Term = '
		AND ISNULL([cmpCoordinateLatitude], '''') <> ''''
		AND ISNULL([cmpCoordinateLongitude], '''') <> ''''

			geometry::STGeomFromText(''POINT('' + CAST([cmpCoordinateLatitude] AS NVARCHAR(100)) + '' '' + CAST([cmpCoordinateLongitude] AS NVARCHAR(100)) + '')'', 0)) = 1
	'

ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN

		 C.CompanyID

				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP

		,C.[cmpReferUserId]

		,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''') + '' '' +

			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''')  + '' '' +

				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP

		AND ISNULL([cmpCoordinateLongitude], '''') <> ''''

			geometry::STGeomFromText(''POINT('' + CAST([cmpCoordinateLatitude] AS NVARCHAR(100)) + '' '' + CAST([cmpCoordinateLongitude] AS NVARCHAR(100)) + '')'', 0)) = 1
	'

/*--*/

	SET @FROM =

			LEFT JOIN

					ON C.CompanyID = P.prsCompanyId	
	  WHERE 1=1 '

/*--*/

FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 20
AND UserId = @UserId

WHERE TypeId = 10
AND UserId = @UserId

	UserId,	TXT, TypeId)
 VALUES ( @UserId, ' SELECT CompanyId ' + @from + @Term, 10 )

(		
/* 
	exec MobspSelectCompanyProjectFromRemindDate '13951210', '13951210', '2017-01-01', '2019-08-19', 0, 1 ,1,10, 1065, 1065,3
*/
@FDate varchar(8),
@TDate varchar(8),
@FDateTime datetime,
@TDateTime datetime,
@prjIsFinished bit,
@prjcnvsStatusGroupId int,
@PageNumber INT = 1,
@PageSize INT = 10,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL ,
@UserId INT = NULL
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
DECLARE @Qry VARCHAR(MAX) = ''
if(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SET @Qry='
		;WITH CtePaging
		AS (
		SELECT 
		Row_number() OVER(order by prjRemindDateTime desc,  prjCompanyId, prjOrderView DESC, ProjectID) AS RowNumber, /*AI14021005 - Change orderView in project add desc to prjOrderView*/
		ProjectID projectId,
		prjCompanyId,
		(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '''')+'' ''+ISNULL(LName, '''') FROM dbo.Users u WHERE U.UserID = (SELECT cmpReferUserId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = prjCompanyId ))cmpReferUser,
		(SELECT cmpReferUserId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = prjCompanyId )cmpReferUserId,

		(SELECT cmpCode FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = prjCompanyId )cmpCode,
		(SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = (SELECT cmpTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = prjCompanyId	) ) cmpTitle, 
		(SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = prjCompanyId) cmpName,
		ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = (SELECT cmpTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = prjCompanyId	)) , '''') + '' ''+
				ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = prjCompanyId), '''') companyName, 
		(SELECT cmpCountryId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = prjCompanyId)cmpCountryId,
		(SELECT Country FROM dbo.Country WHERE CountryID = (SELECT cmpCountryId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = prjCompanyId ))cmpCountry,
		(SELECT cmpProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = prjCompanyId)cmpProvinceId,					 	
		(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province WHERE ProvinceID = (SELECT cmpProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = prjCompanyId ))cmpProvince,

		(SELECT City FROM dbo.City WHERE CityID = (SELECT cmpCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = prjCompanyId ))cmpCity,	
		(SELECT cmpActivityType FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID =prjCompanyId	)cmpActivityType,	
		(SELECT Presentation FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPresentation WHERE CompanyPresentationID = (SELECT cmpPresentationId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = prjCompanyId) )cmpPresentation,
		(SELECT CompanyTypeName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyType WHERE CompanyTypeID = (SELECT cmpTypeId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = prjCompanyId) )cmpType,	
		(SELECT GroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroups WHERE CompanyGroupID = (SELECT cmpGroupId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = prjCompanyId) )cmpGroup,
		(SELECT SubGroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanySubGroups WHERE CompanySubGroupID = (SELECT cmpSubGroupId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = prjCompanyId) )cmpSubGroup,			 			
		(SELECT GroupIIName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroupsII WHERE CompanyGroupIIID = (SELECT cmpGroupIdII FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = prjCompanyId) )cmpGroupII,			 			
		(SELECT GroupIIIName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroupsIII WHERE CompanyGroupIIIID = (SELECT cmpGroupIdIII FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = prjCompanyId) )cmpGroupIII,
		(SELECT GroupIVName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroupsIV WHERE CompanyGroupIVID = (SELECT cmpGroupIdIV FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = prjCompanyId) )cmpGroupIV,
		prjTitle,
		prjTitle + ISNULL('' '' + prjPostFix , '''') prjTitleWithPostFix,
		prjPostFix,
		(SELECT dbo.fnFormatPersianDate(prjRemindDate,''/'')) prjRemindDate,
		prjRemindDateTime,
		prjIsFinished,
		prjDesc,
		(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '''')+'' ''+ISNULL(LName, '''') FROM dbo.Users u WHERE U.UserID = P.FirstRegisterUserId) prjFirstRegisterUser,
		(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '''')+'' ''+ISNULL(LName, '''') FROM dbo.Users u WHERE U.UserID = P.prjStarterUserId) prjStarterUser,
		(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '''')+'' ''+ISNULL(LName, '''') FROM dbo.Users u WHERE U.UserID = prjFinishedUserId) prjFinishedUser,
		(SELECT ProjectGrade FROM dbo.CrmProjectGrade WHERE ProjectGradeID = prjGradeId) prjGrade,
		(SELECT [Priority] FROM dbo.CrmProjectPriority WHERE ProjectPriorityID = prjPriorityId) prjPriority,
		(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '''')+'' ''+ISNULL(LName, '''') FROM dbo.Users u WHERE u.UserID = P.UserId )prjUser,
		(SELECT ProjectGroup FROM dbo.CrmProjectGroups WHERE ProjectGroupID = prjGroupId) prjGroup,
		(SELECT ConversationStatusGroup FROM dbo.CrmConversationStatusGroups WHERE ConversationStatusGroupID = prjcnvsStatusGroupId)prjcnvsStatusGroup,	
		prjcnvsStatusGroupId,
		(SELECT TOP 1 ISNULL(
			(
				SELECT  TOP 1 ProgressPercent 
				FROM dbo.CrmConversationStatus cnvs
				WHERE ConversationStatusId = 
				(
					SELECT  TOP 1 cnvsStatusID
					FROM dbo.CrmConversation
					WHERE cnvsProjectID = P.ProjectID
					ORDER BY ConversationID DESC
				)				
		), 0) ) [progressPercent]
	FROM crmprojects P 
	WHERE prjRemindDateTime between ''' + CAST(@FDateTime AS VARCHAR(50))+ ''' AND ''' + CAST(@TDateTime AS VARCHAR(50))+ ''' 
	AND	P.[prjStarterUserId] IN 
		(SELECT ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(50)) +' UNION 
		SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(50)) +' AND LinkedTypeId = 24)
	 AND ISNULL(prjIsFinished, 0) = ' + CAST(@prjIsFinished AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
	AND prjcnvsStatusGroupId = ' + CAST(@prjcnvsStatusGroupId AS VARCHAR(50))+ '
	AND P.[prjTitle] NOT IN (SELECT ForbiddenProjectTitle 
						FROM dbo.CrmProjectsForbiddenForUser
						WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(50))+ ')
	AND P.prjCompanyId '	
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SET @Qry = '
		;WITH CtePaging
		 AS (
		SELECT 
		Row_number() OVER(order by prjRemindDateTime desc,  prjCompanyId, prjOrderView DESC, ProjectID) AS RowNumber,
		ProjectID projectId,
		prjCompanyId,
		(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '''')+'' ''+ISNULL(LName, '''') FROM dbo.Users_Lang U WHERE U.UserID = (SELECT cmpReferUserId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = prjCompanyId ) and U.LanguageID= ' +CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50))+') cmpReferUser,
		(SELECT cmpReferUserId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = prjCompanyId )cmpReferUserId,
		(SELECT cmpNumber FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = prjCompanyId 	)cmpNumber,
		(SELECT cmpCode FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = prjCompanyId )cmpCode,

			cmpTln.CompanyTitleID = (SELECT cmpTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompany pp WHERE pp.CompanyID = prjCompanyId ) AND cmpTln.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + ') , '''') + '' ''+ 
			ISNULL((select [cmpName] from CRMCompany_Lang CMPl where CMPl.CompanyID = prjCompanyId AND CMPl.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + '), '''') companyName,

		(SELECT ProjectGroup FROM dbo.CrmProjectGroups_Lang pgln WHERE pgln.ProjectGroupID = prjGroupId AND pgln.LanguageID=' +CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50))+') prjGroup,
		(SELECT ConversationStatusGroup FROM dbo.CrmConversationStatusGroups_Lang  cslan WHERE cslan.ConversationStatusGroupID = prjcnvsStatusGroupId and cslan.LanguageID=' +CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50))+') prjcnvsStatusGroup,

	AND P.[prjTitle] NOT IN (SELECT ForbiddenProjectTitle 
						FROM dbo.CrmProjectsForbiddenForUser
						WHERE CrmUserId = '+ CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(50)) + ') 
	AND P.prjCompanyId '

DECLARE @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(max), @paging VARCHAR(MAX)
EXEC dbo.CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @UserId
SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT
FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 20
AND UserId = @UserId
/*--*/

SET @paging = '
	)
	SELECT 
		* , 
		(SELECT 
			CASE  WHEN (Count(*) - ((Count(*) / ' +  CAST(@PageSize AS VARCHAR(50)) + ') *  ' + CAST(@PageSize AS VARCHAR(50)) + ') ) = 0					
			THEN
				Count(*) /  '  + CAST(@PageSize AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
			ELSE
				(Count(*) / ' + CAST(@PageSize AS VARCHAR(50)) + ') + 1
			END
		FROM CtePaging) AS AllPage
	FROM   CtePaging 
	WHERE  RowNumber BETWEEN (' + CAST(@PageNumber AS VARCHAR(50)) + ' - 1 ) * ' + CAST(@PageSize AS VARCHAR(50)) + ' + 1 AND ' + CAST(@PageNumber AS VARCHAR(50)) + ' * ' +  CAST(@PageSize AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''

EXECUTE(@Qry + ' IN (SELECT CompanyId FROM (SELECT CompanyId FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE 1 = 1 ' + @cmpDataPermision + ') T ) ' + @paging)

/**/
END

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspSelectCompanyWithID]
(	
/* 
	EXEC [MobspSelectCompanyWithID]  69103, 3, 1033, 1065
*/
@CompanyId INT,
/*RZ960615- Add permission data*/
@UserId INT,
/*RZ960411 For multi language*/
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
AS 
BEGIN
/**/
/*RZ960615- Add permission data*/
DECLARE @Qry VARCHAR(MAX)
/*RZ961123- Add multi group*/
DECLARE @cmpMultiGroupsId NVARCHAR(MAX) = N'', @cmpMultiGroupsName NVARCHAR(MAX)=N''
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SELECT  
		@cmpMultiGroupsId = @cmpMultiGroupsId + CAST(CMG.[CompanyGroupsMultiID] AS NVARCHAR(20)) + '|',
		@cmpMultiGroupsName = @cmpMultiGroupsName + (SELECT GM.[CompanyGroupsMultiName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyGroupsMulti] GM WHERE GM.[CompanyGroupsMultiID] = CMG.[CompanyGroupsMultiID]) + '|'  
	FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyMultiGroups] CMG WHERE CMG.[CompanyID] = @CompanyId
END 
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SELECT  
		@cmpMultiGroupsId = @cmpMultiGroupsId + CAST(CMG.[CompanyGroupsMultiID] AS NVARCHAR(20)) + '|',
		@cmpMultiGroupsName = @cmpMultiGroupsName + (SELECT GM.[CompanyGroupsMultiName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyGroupsMulti_Lang] GM WHERE GM.[CompanyGroupsMultiID] = CMG.[CompanyGroupsMultiID] AND GM.LanguageID = @LanguageID) + '|'  
	FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyMultiGroups] CMG WHERE CMG.[CompanyID] = @CompanyId
END
/**/
IF(@cmpMultiGroupsId IS NOT NULL AND @cmpMultiGroupsId <> '')
	SET @cmpMultiGroupsId = ISNULL(LEFT(@cmpMultiGroupsId, LEN(@cmpMultiGroupsId) - 1) ,'')
ELSE
SET @cmpMultiGroupsId = N''
IF(@cmpMultiGroupsName IS NOT NULL AND @cmpMultiGroupsName <> '')
    SET @cmpMultiGroupsName = ISNULL(LEFT(@cmpMultiGroupsName, LEN(@cmpMultiGroupsName) - 1) ,'')
ELSE
	SET @cmpMultiGroupsName =N''

/***************************/
/*AI14010529*/

SELECT

IF(ISNULL(@cmpCampaignIds, '') <> '')
BEGIN
	IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
		SELECT
			@cmpCampaignsName = @cmpCampaignsName + (SELECT [CampaignName] FROM dbo.CrmCompanyCampaign where [CompanyCampaignId] = CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT)) + ','
		FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](@cmpCampaignIds, ',')
	END
	ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
		SELECT
			@cmpCampaignsName = @cmpCampaignsName + (SELECT [CampaignName] FROM dbo.CrmCompanyCampaign where [CompanyCampaignId] = CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT)) + ','
		FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](@cmpCampaignIds, ',')
	END

	IF(@cmpCampaignsName IS NOT NULL AND @cmpCampaignsName <> '')
		SET @cmpCampaignsName = ISNULL(LEFT(@cmpCampaignsName, LEN(@cmpCampaignsName) - 1) ,'')
END

/***************************/
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)

/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*set date permision*/
DECLARE @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(MAX)
EXEC [dbo].[CRMspSetcmpDataPermission] @UserId
SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT
FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 20
AND UserId = @UserId
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*--------------------------- Condition -----------------------------*/
IF @LetterId > 0
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @Term = ' AND LetterId = ' + CAST(@LetterId AS VARCHAR(MAX)) 
	SET @OrderBy = ' Order By LetterNumber DESC'
/**/
END
ELSE IF(@FDateTime IS NOT NULL AND @TDateTime IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @Term = ' AND LetterTypeId = ' + CAST(@LetterTypeId AS VARCHAR(MAX)) +
				' AND LetterDateTime  BETWEEN ''' + CAST(@FDateTime AS VARCHAR(50))  + ''' AND ''' + CAST(@TDateTime AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''''
	SET @OrderBy = ' Order By case when LetterTypeId = 1 then LetterNumber else LetterNumberII end DESC'
/**/
END
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*-------------------------- Linked Type-----------------------------*/
SET @Term = @Term + ' AND L.[FirstRegisterUserId] IN 
					(SELECT ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ' UNION  
					SELECT LinkedUserId FROM [dbo].[CrmLinkedUsers] 
					WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 26 ) ' + 
					' AND L.LetterCompanyId IN (SELECT C.CompanyID FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] C WHERE 1 = 1 ' + ISNULL(@cmpDataPermision,'') + ' )'
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/

/**/
IF(@PageNumber > 0 AND @LetterId = 0)
BEGIN
	SET @FinalQry = 
			';WITH CtePaging  AS (' + 
			'SELECT 						
			*,
			Row_number() OVER('+ @OrderBy +') AS RowNum,
			CASE  WHEN (COUNT(*) OVER() - ((COUNT(*) OVER() / ' + CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ') * ' + CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ') ) = 0
				THEN
					COUNT(*) OVER() / ' + CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '
				ELSE
					(COUNT(*) OVER() / ' + CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ') + 1
				END
			AS AllPage
			FROM(' + 
				@Qry + @From + @Term
			+') AS temTbl' 													
			+') 
			SELECT 
				* 	
			FROM   CtePaging 
			WHERE  RowNum BETWEEN ( ' + CAST(@PageNumber AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '- 1 ) * ' + CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' + 1 AND ' + CAST(@PageNumber AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' * ' + CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' ORDER BY RowNum'
END
ELSE
BEGIN
	SET @FinalQry = @Qry + '
							,Row_number() OVER(' + @OrderBy + ') AS RowNum
							,1 AS AllPage' + @From + @Term + ' ORDER BY RowNum'
END
/**/

EXECUTE(@FinalQry)

/**/
END

-- [dbo].[MobspSetcmpDataPermission] | Exact target







/*RZ961205*/
/*AI14000621 - Skip the operation for userId < 0*/
/*AI14000710 - Remove skip the operation for userId < 0 because of adding a record for user -20 at dbo.UserPermission*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspSetcmpDataPermission]
(		
/* 
	EXEC MobspSetcmpDataPermission 3 
*/
@UserId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
	DECLARE @Term VARCHAR(4000)
	SET @Term = '';
	/*IF(@UserId > 0) /*AI14000621 - IF added*/
	BEGIN AI14000710*/
	/*--Province*/
	IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenProvince
	WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND C.cmpProvinceId Not IN (
	SELECT ForbiddenProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenProvince
	WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
	IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessProvince
	WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND C.cmpProvinceId IN (
	SELECT JustAccessProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessProvince
	WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
	/*--City*/
	IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCity
	WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND C.cmpCityId Not IN (
	SELECT ForbiddenCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCity
	WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
	IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessCity
	WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND C.cmpCityId IN (
	SELECT JustAccessCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessCity
	WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
	/*--GroupIV*/
	IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroupIV
	WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND IsNull(C.cmpGroupIdIV, 0) Not IN (
	SELECT ForbiddenGroupIVId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroupIV
	WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
	IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessGroupIV
	WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND IsNull(C.cmpGroupIdIV, 0) IN (
	SELECT JustAccessGroupIVId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessGroupIV
	WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
	/*--Company*/
	IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCompany
	WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND C.CompanyId Not IN (
	SELECT ForbiddenCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCompany
	WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
	IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessCompany
	WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND C.CompanyId IN (
	SELECT JustAccessCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessCompany
	WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
	/*--Group*/
	IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroup
	WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND IsNull(C.cmpGroupId, 0) Not IN (
	SELECT ForbiddenGroupId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroup
	WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
	IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessGroup
	WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND IsNull(C.cmpGroupId, 0) IN (
	SELECT JustAccessGroupId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessGroup
	WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
	/*--SubGroup*/
	IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenSubGroup
	WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND IsNull(C.cmpSubGroupId, 0) Not IN (
	SELECT ForbiddenSubGroupId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenSubGroup
	WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
	IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessSubGroup
	WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND IsNull(C.cmpSubGroupId, 0) IN (
	SELECT JustAccessSubGroupId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessSubGroup
	WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
	/*--*/
	/*--cmpReferUser*/
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' And C.cmpReferUserId IN( 
	(SELECT '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10)) + ' UNION 
	SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = '+ 
	CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 14 ) ) '

	/*END AI14000710-End*/
	/*
	--
	--
	--
	--
	*/
	DELETE dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
	WHERE TypeId = 20
	AND UserId = @UserId
	/*--*/
	INSERT INTO dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo (
		UserId,	TXT, TypeId)
	 VALUES ( @UserId, @Term, 20 ) 


END

/******************************* CHECK Access comapny and datapermission *************************/
/*RZ971222- Add check access to cmp and datapermission*/
/**/
IF(@CompanyId IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @IsAccessCompanyId BIT = 0

	EXEC dbo.MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 	
	@Mode = 8,
	@Id = @CompanyId,
	@CrmUserId = @UserId,
	@IsAccess = @IsAccessCompanyId OUTPUT
				
	IF(@IsAccessCompanyId = 0)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @FoundCmpName NVARCHAR(2000)
		SET @FoundCmpName = (SELECT
							CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
								THEN (SELECT  
										ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId),'') + ' ' +
										ISNULL(cmpName,'')
								FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @CompanyId)
								ELSE (SELECT  
										ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' +
										ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CL WHERE CL.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CL.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
										FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @CompanyId)
							END
							)
							
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=CompanyAccess|' + ISNULL(@FoundCmpName,'') + '|Msg=CompanyAccess|'
	END	 
END
/**/
IF(@cmpGroupId IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @IsAccessGroupId BIT = 0

	EXEC dbo.MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 	
	@Mode = 3,
	@Id = @cmpGroupId,
	@CrmUserId = @UserId,
	@IsAccess = @IsAccessGroupId OUTPUT
				
	IF(@IsAccessGroupId = 0)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @GroupName NVARCHAR(1000)
		SET @GroupName = (SELECT
							CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
								THEN (SELECT GroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroups CG WHERE CG.CompanyGroupID = @cmpGroupId)
								ELSE (SELECT GroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroups_Lang CG WHERE CG.CompanyGroupID = @cmpGroupId AND CG.LanguageID = @LanguageID)
							END
							)

		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=CmpGroupIdAccess|' + ISNULL(@GroupName,'') + '|Msg=CmpGroupIdAccess|'
	END	 
END
/**/
IF(@cmpSubGroupId IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @IsAccessSubGroupId BIT = 0

	EXEC dbo.MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 	
	@Mode = 4,
	@Id = @cmpSubGroupId,
	@CrmUserId = @UserId,
	@IsAccess = @IsAccessSubGroupId OUTPUT
				
	IF(@IsAccessSubGroupId = 0)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @SubGroupName NVARCHAR(1000)
		SET @SubGroupName = (SELECT
							CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
								THEN (SELECT SubGroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanySubGroups CG WHERE CG.CompanySubGroupID = @cmpSubGroupId)
								ELSE (SELECT SubGroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanySubGroups_Lang CG WHERE CG.CompanySubGroupID = @cmpSubGroupId AND CG.LanguageID = @LanguageID)
							END
							)

		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=CmpSubGroupIdAccess|' + ISNULL(@SubGroupName,'') + '|Msg=CmpSubGroupIdAccess|'
					
	END																				
END
/**/
IF(@cmpGroupIdIV IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @IsAccessGroupIdIVId BIT = 0

	EXEC dbo.MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 	
	@Mode = 5,
	@Id = @cmpGroupIdIV,
	@CrmUserId = @UserId,
	@IsAccess = @IsAccessGroupIdIVId OUTPUT
				
	IF(@IsAccessGroupIdIVId = 0)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @GroupIVName NVARCHAR(1000)

		SET @GroupIVName = (SELECT
							CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
								THEN (SELECT GroupIVName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroupsIV CG WHERE CG.CompanyGroupIVID = @cmpGroupIdIV)
								ELSE (SELECT GroupIVName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroupsIV_Lang CG WHERE CG.CompanyGroupIVID = @cmpGroupIdIV AND CG.LanguageID = @LanguageID)
							END
							)
										
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=CmpGroupIdIVAccess|' + ISNULL(@GroupIVName,'') + '|Msg=CmpGroupIdIVAccess|'
	END
END

IF(@cmpProvinceId IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @IsAccessProvinceId BIT = 0

	EXEC dbo.MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 	
	@Mode = 1,
	@Id = @cmpProvinceId,
	@CrmUserId = @UserId,
	@IsAccess = @IsAccessProvinceId OUTPUT
				
	IF(@IsAccessProvinceId = 0)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @ProvinceName NVARCHAR(1000)
		SET @ProvinceName = (SELECT
							CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
								THEN (SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province P WHERE P.ProvinceID = @cmpProvinceId)
								ELSE (SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province_Lang P WHERE P.ProvinceID = @cmpProvinceId AND P.LanguageID = @LanguageID)
							END
							)
					
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=CmpProvinceIdAccess|' + ISNULL(@ProvinceName,'') + '|Msg=CmpProvinceIdAccess|'
	END																																				 	 
END

	@Mode = 2,
	@Id = @cmpCityId,
	@CrmUserId = @UserId,
	@IsAccess = @IsAccessCityId OUTPUT
				
	IF(@IsAccessCityId = 0)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @CityName NVARCHAR(1000)

							CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
								THEN (SELECT City FROM dbo.City C WHERE C.CityID = @cmpCityId)
								ELSE (SELECT City FROM dbo.City_Lang C WHERE C.CityID = @cmpCityId AND C.LanguageID = @LanguageID)
							END
							)					

		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=CmpCityIdAccess|' + ISNULL(@CityName,'') + '|Msg=CmpCityIdAccess|'
	END																					 
END

/************************ Make A Table For check duplicate ***************************/
DECLARE @TmpTableForCheckDuplicate TABLE (
	[CompanyID] INT,	
	[cmpId] INT,	
	[cmpName] NVARCHAR(MAX),
	[Title] NVARCHAR(MAX),	
	[CompanyWithTitle] NVARCHAR(MAX),	
	[cmpTel] NVARCHAR(50),
	[cmpTelII] NVARCHAR(50),	
	[cmpFax] NVARCHAR(50),
	[Country] NVARCHAR(MAX),
	[Provice] NVARCHAR(MAX),
	[City] NVARCHAR(MAX),	
	[cmpAddress] NVARCHAR(MAX),
	[prsName] NVARCHAR(MAX),
	[prsTel] NVARCHAR(50),
	[prsTelII] NVARCHAR(50),
	[prsFax] NVARCHAR(50),
	[prsMobile] NVARCHAR(50),
	[prsEmail] NVARCHAR(300),
	[prsEmailII] NVARCHAR(300),
	[cmpNumber] INT,	
	[ReferUser] NVARCHAR(MAX)			
)
DECLARE @DupCompanyId INT , @DupCmpTitle NVARCHAR(MAX), @DupCmpName NVARCHAR(MAX), @DupCmpCity NVARCHAR(MAX), @DupCmpNumber INT
/******************************* cmpName with city and title *************************/
IF(@ErrorMessage = '' OR @ErrorMessage IS NULL)
BEGIN
	IF(@cmpName IS NOT NULL AND @cmpName <> '' AND @cmpName <> @PersonLabel)
	BEGIN
		INSERT INTO @TmpTableForCheckDuplicate 
		EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCompanyForSetReason]
				@Type  = 1, 
				@Id = @CompanyId, 
				@Val = @cmpName, 
				@TitleID = @cmpTitleId, 
				@CityId = @cmpCityId, 
				@UserID  = @UserId,
				@LackOfShowText = @LackOfShowText,
				@LanguageID  = @LanguageID,
				@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

		SELECT TOP 1
			@DupCompanyId = [CompanyID],
			@DupCmpTitle = [Title], 
			@DupCmpName = [cmpName], 
			@DupCmpCity = [City], 
			@DupCmpNumber = [cmpNumber]
		FROM @TmpTableForCheckDuplicate	 
		
		IF(@DupCompanyId IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '@cmpName=Dup,' + '@DupCompanyId='+ ISNULL(CAST(@DupCompanyId AS NVARCHAR(10)),'') + 
			',@DupCmpTitle='+ ISNULL(@DupCmpTitle,'') + ',@DupCmpName=' + ISNULL(@DupCmpName,'') + ',@DupCmpCity=' + ISNULL(@DupCmpCity,'') +
			',@DupCmpNumber=' + ISNULL(CAST(@DupCmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(10)),'') + '|^|'	
		END			
	END
	ELSE IF(@cmpName IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '@cmpName=IsNull'+ '|^|'
END			
/**/
/*AI990719 - below checks added*/
/******************************* cmpCode *********************************************/
IF(@cmpCode IS NOT NULL AND @cmpCode <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	IF EXISTS (
	SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
	WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' )
	AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actCmpCodeNotDup'
						AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' ))
	AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
	BEGIN
	/**/
		DECLARE @result NVARCHAR(700) = ''
		EXEC MobspCheckDuplicateCompanyCode
		@cmpCode = @cmpCode, 
		@CurrCmpID = @CompanyId,
		@LanguageID  = @LanguageID,
		@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID ,
		@RecordCount = @result OUTPUT 
					
		IF(@result IS NOT NULL AND @result <> '')
		BEGIN
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '@cmpCode=Dup,@cmpName='+ @result + '|^|'	
		END		
	/**/
	END
/**/
END
/******************************* cmpTel *********************************************/

						AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' ))
	AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
	SET @HavePermissionDuplicatecmpTel = 1

	IF(@HavePermissionDuplicatecmpTel = 0 AND @CantSaveDupTel = 1)

	/**/
