BEGIN
	UPDATE [dbo].[CrmCompany] 
		SET 
		 [cmpName] = @cmpName
		,[cmpNameEng] = @cmpNameEng
		,[cmpTitleId] = @cmpTitleId
		,[cmpFame] = @cmpFame
		,[cmpActivityType] = @cmpActivityType
		,[cmpGroupId] = @cmpGroupId
		,[cmpSubGroupId] = @cmpSubGroupId
		,[cmpGroupIdII] = @cmpGroupIdII
		,[cmpGroupIdIII] = @cmpGroupIdIII
		,[cmpGroupIdIV] = @cmpGroupIdIV
		,[cmpTypeId] = @cmpTypeId
		,[cmpPresentationId] = @cmpPresentationId
		,[cmpPreTel] = @cmpPreTel
		,[cmpTel] = @cmpTel
		,[cmpTelUpTo] = @cmpTelUpTo
		,[cmpTelDesc] = @cmpTelDesc
		,[cmpPreTelII] = @cmpPreTelII
		,[cmpTelII] = @cmpTelII
		,[cmpTelUpToII] = @cmpTelUpToII
		,[cmpTelIIDesc] = @cmpTelIIDesc
		,[cmpMobile] = @cmpMobile
		,[cmpFax] = @cmpFax
		,[cmpFaxDesc] = @cmpFaxDesc
		,[cmpCountryId] = @cmpCountryId
		,[cmpProvinceId] = @cmpProvinceId
		,[cmpCityId] = @cmpCityId
		,[cmpAddress] = @cmpAddress
		,[cmpAddressDesc] = @cmpAddressDesc
		,[cmpZipCode] = @cmpZipCode
		,[cmpPostBox] = @cmpPostBox
		,[cmpWebSite] = @cmpWebSite
		,[cmpEmail] = @cmpEmail
		,[cmpInteresting] = @cmpInteresting
		,[cmpSpecialPoint] = @cmpSpecialPoint
		,[cmpDescription] = @cmpDescription
		/*,[cmpUserName] = @cmpUserName
		,[cmpPassword] = @cmpPassword AI14020220*/
		,[UserId] = @UserId
		,[Year1] = @Year1
		,[Month1] = @Month1
		,[RegisterDate] = @RegisterDate
		,[RegisterDatePersian] = @RegisterDatePersian		
		,[cmpRegistrationNumber] = @cmpRegistrationNumber
		,[cmpEconomicCode] = @cmpEconomicCode
		,[cmpNationalCode] = @cmpNationalCode      
		,[cmpBuyLimit] = @cmpBuyLimit
		,[cmpAccountNumber] = @cmpAccountNumber
		,[cmpAccInquiry] = @cmpAccInquiry
		,[cmpFreight] = @cmpFreight
		,[cmpInquiry] = @cmpInquiry
		,[cmpReferUserId] = @cmpReferUserId
		,[AppType] = @AppType
		,[cmpSpecialWordForCI] = @cmpSpecialWordForCI
		,[cmpCode] = @cmpCode
		,[cmpEffectiveDatePersian] = @cmpEffectiveDatePersian
		,[cmpEffectiveDateTime] = @cmpEffectiveDateTime	      
		,[cmpCoordinateLatitude] = @cmpCoordinateLatitude
		,[cmpCoordinateLongitude] = @cmpCoordinateLongitude
		,[cmpCoordinateLastDate] = @cmpCoordinateLastDate /*AI14010621 - @cmpCoordinateLastDate*/
		,[cmpCoordinateLastDatePersian] = @cmpCoordinateLastDatePersian /*AI14010621 - @cmpCoordinateLastDatePersian*/
		,[cmpCoordinateZoomLevel] = @cmpCoordinateZoomLevel
		,[cmpCoordinateLastUserId] = @cmpCoordinateLastUserId
		,[cmpIsDraft] = @cmpIsDraft /*AI990827*/
		,[cmpKind] = @cmpKind /*AI990827*/
		,[cmpMainPersonId] = @cmpMainPersonId /*AI990827*/
		,[cmpCampaignIds] = @cmpCampaignIds /*AI14010529*/
		/*AI14020428*/
		,[cmpNameOfficial] = @cmpNameOfficial
		,[cmpSuppUserId] = @cmpSuppUserId
		,[cmpOfficialCityId] = @cmpOfficialCityId
		,[cmpOfficialAddress] = @cmpOfficialAddress
		,[cmpOfficialZipCode] = @cmpOfficialZipCode
		,[cmpOfficialDesc] = @cmpOfficialDesc
		,[cmpOfficialTel] = @cmpOfficialTel
		,[TspCmpTob] = @cmpTspCmpTob
		,[TspCmpBbc] = @cmpTspCmpBbc
		,[TspCmpBpn] = @cmpTspCmpBpn
		/*AI14020428-End*/
	WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId
	/*********** Update to lang table ***********/
	UPDATE [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang]
	   SET 
		   [cmpName] = @cmpName
		  ,[cmpFame] = @cmpFame
		  ,[cmpActivityType] = @cmpActivityType
		  ,[cmpTelDesc] = @cmpTelDesc
		  ,[cmpTelIIDesc] = @cmpTelIIDesc
		  ,[cmpFaxDesc] = @cmpFaxDesc
		  ,[cmpAddress] = @cmpAddress
		  ,[cmpAddressDesc] = @cmpAddressDesc
		  ,[cmpInteresting] = @cmpInteresting
		  ,[cmpSpecialPoint] = @cmpSpecialPoint
		  ,[cmpDescription] = @cmpDescription
		  ,[cmpSpecialWordForCI] = @cmpSpecialWordForCI
		  ,[cmpInquiry] = @cmpInquiry
	 WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId
	 AND [LanguageID] IN (SELECT LN.LanguageID FROM dbo.Languages LN 
							WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
							AND LN.IsActive = 1)		
	/******* Insert to multi group table ********/
	IF(@cmpMultiGroups IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		/************ For add group *************/
		DECLARE @GroupId INT
		DECLARE CrsrMultiGroup CURSOR FOR
			SELECT splitdata 
			FROM dbo.fnSplitString(@cmpMultiGroups, '|')		
		/**/
		OPEN CrsrMultiGroup
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrMultiGroup INTO @GroupId
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
			/***** Find group multi for the company  *****/
			DECLARE @CompanyGroupsMultiId INT
			SET @CompanyGroupsMultiId = (SELECT TOP 1 CompanyGroupsMultiID FROM dbo.CrmCompanyMultiGroups
											WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId
											AND CompanyGroupsMultiID = CAST(@GroupId AS INT))
			/*IF multi group is not then add*/
			IF(@CompanyGroupsMultiId IS NULL)
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyMultiGroups]
				   (
					[CompanyID]
				   ,[CompanyGroupsMultiID]
				   ,[UserId]
				   ,[Year1]
				   ,[Month1]
				   ,[RegisterDate]
				   ,[RegisterDatePersian]
				   ,[AppType]
				   ,[LanguageID]
				   ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				   ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				   ,[FirstRegisterDate]
					)
				VALUES
				   (
					@CompanyId
				   ,CAST(@GroupId AS INT)
				   ,@UserId
				   ,@Year1
				   ,@Month1
				   ,@RegisterDate
				   ,@RegisterDatePersian
				   ,@AppType
				   ,@DefaultLanguageID
				   ,@UserId
				   ,@RegisterDatePersian
				   ,@RegisterDate
				   )
			END																							 					
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrMultiGroup INTO @GroupId
		END
		CLOSE CrsrMultiGroup
		DEALLOCATE CrsrMultiGroup
		/************ For delete group *************/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyMultiGroups]
					WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId
					AND CompanyGroupsMultiID NOT IN(SELECT CAST(splitdata AS INT) 
														FROM dbo.fnSplitString(@cmpMultiGroups, '|'))				
	END				 	 	 
END
/**/
IF (@@ERROR > 0)
BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Update Company' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
ELSE 
COMMIT TRAN 
/**/
SET @UpdatedCompanyId = @CompanyId
/**/
IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
BEGIN 
	RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
 	SELECT 0
END 
ELSE
BEGIN
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	SELECT 1
END	

IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
BEGIN 
	RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
 	SELECT 0
END 
ELSE
BEGIN
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	SELECT 1
END	
/**/
END







GO
-- [dbo].[MobspUpdateCompanyForPs] | References permission/temp-filter machinery



/*AI990630 - pre tel added*/
/*AI990321*/
/*این اس پی قرار است پاک شود و ای پی ای ادیت شود و یک اس پی جدید نوشته شود که برای تمام جاها استفاده شود*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspUpdateCompanyForPs]
/*
	exec MobspUpdateCompanyForPs  		
*/
(
@CompanyId INT,
@Company NVARCHAR(MAX) ,
@UserId INT,
@Year1 SMALLINT,
@AppType VARCHAR(20), 
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
@PersonLabel NVARCHAR(50),
@LackOfShowText NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
/**/
@UpdatedCompanyId INT OUTPUT,
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** TRANSACTION *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/***************************** Define fields ************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
DECLARE @IsDuplicate BIT,
/*********** Company ***********/
/*AI990630 - pre tel added*/
@cmpPreTel NVARCHAR(10),
@cmpTel NVARCHAR(15),
@cmpCountryId INT,
@cmpProvinceId INT,
@cmpCityId INT,
@cmpAddress NVARCHAR(250),
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@RegisterTime NVARCHAR(8),
@cmpCoordinateLatitude NVARCHAR(100),
@cmpCoordinateLongitude NVARCHAR(100),
@cmpCoordinateLastDate DATETIME,
@cmpCoordinateLastDatePersian VARCHAR(8),
@cmpCoordinateZoomLevel INT,
@cmpCoordinateLastUserId INT,
@cmpMultiGroups NVARCHAR(500)
/************************************ Error *************************************/
DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT
/********************************************************************************/
/***************************** Split fields *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
SET @IsDuplicate  = 0
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Company *****************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRAN
/**/
/************************* Find record from table *******************************/
DECLARE @FindRecord INT
SET @FindRecord = (SELECT TOP 1 C.CompanyID FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @CompanyId) 
/**/
IF(@Company IS NOT NULL AND @Company <> '' AND @CompanyId > 0 AND @FindRecord > 0 AND @FindRecord IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
DECLARE @CmpField NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
WHILE LEN(@Company) > 0
BEGIN
    IF PATINDEX('%|#|%', @Company) > 0
    BEGIN
        SET @CmpField = SUBSTRING(@Company,
                                    0,
                                    PATINDEX('%|#|%', @Company))
        /*SELECT @CmpField*/
        /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
        /*Get parameter name and split value form parameter*/
        /*For example => cmpTypeId=102 => cmpTypeId= => cmpTypeId => Compare => value*/
        DECLARE @CmpFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
        DECLARE @CmpFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
        SET @CmpFieldName = SUBSTRING(@CmpField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@CmpField)-1)                                    
		SET @CmpFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@CmpField, CHARINDEX('=', @CmpField)+1, Len(@CmpField))
        /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
        /*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill variabele ------------------------------*/
        /*BEGIN COMPANY*/
		/*AI990630 - pre tel added*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpPreTel'
        BEGIN
			SET @cmpPreTel = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END
        END
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpTel'
        BEGIN
			SET @cmpTel = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpCountryId'
        BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpCountryId = CASE WHEN CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) = -1 THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpProvinceId'
        BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpProvinceId = CASE WHEN CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) = -1 THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpCityId'
        BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpCityId = CASE WHEN CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) = -1 THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpAddress'
        BEGIN
			SET @cmpAddress = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/		        
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpCoordinateLatitude'
        BEGIN
			SET @cmpCoordinateLatitude = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/		
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpCoordinateLongitude'
        BEGIN
			SET @cmpCoordinateLongitude = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpCoordinateLastDatePersian'
        BEGIN
			SET @cmpCoordinateLastDatePersian = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE REPLACE(@CmpFieldValue, '/', '') END							
			SET @cmpCoordinateLastDate = CASE WHEN @cmpCoordinateLastDatePersian IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@cmpCoordinateLastDatePersian, null) END        	
        END
       /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpCoordinateZoomLevel'
        BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpCoordinateZoomLevel = CASE WHEN CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) = -1 THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpCoordinateLastUserId'
        BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpCoordinateLastUserId = CASE WHEN CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) = -1 THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
        IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpMultiGroups'
		BEGIN
			SET @cmpMultiGroups = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END	
		END 
		/*--------------------------*/		
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'RegisterDatePersian'
        BEGIN
			SET @RegisterDatePersian = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE REPLACE(@CmpFieldValue, '/', '') END							
			SET @RegisterDate = CASE WHEN @RegisterDatePersian IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@RegisterDatePersian, null) END	
			SET @Month1 = CASE WHEN @RegisterDatePersian = NULL THEN NULL ELSE CAST(SUBSTRING(@RegisterDatePersian,5,2) AS SMALLINT) END											 	
        END   
        /*--------------------------*/		
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'RegisterTime'
        BEGIN
			SET @RegisterTime = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END										
        END
        /*END COMPANY*/		
        /*END GENERAL*/
        /*---------------------------- END Fill variabele --------------------------------*/
        /*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/
        SET @Company = SUBSTRING(@Company,
                                  LEN(@CmpField + '|#|') + 1,
                                  LEN(@Company))
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SET @CmpField = @Company
        SET @Company = NULL        
    END
END
/************************** Set time ********************************************/
IF(@RegisterTime IS NOT NULL AND @RegisterDatePersian IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	SET @RegisterDate = CASE WHEN @RegisterDatePersian IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@RegisterDatePersian, @RegisterTime) END			 	
END
/****/		
IF(@RegisterTime IS NOT NULL AND @cmpCoordinateLastDatePersian IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	SET @cmpCoordinateLastDate = CASE WHEN @cmpCoordinateLastDatePersian IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@cmpCoordinateLastDatePersian, @RegisterTime) END			 	
END
/*********************************************************************************/
END
ELSE
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=0' 	
/**/
IF (@@ERROR > 0)
BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Input Company' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
ELSE 
COMMIT TRAN 
/**/
/*==============================================================================*/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Update Company **********************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRAN
/**/
/******************************* CHECK Access comapny and datapermission *************************/
/*RZ971222- Add check access to cmp and datapermission*/
/**/
IF(@CompanyId IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @IsAccessCompanyId BIT = 0

	EXEC dbo.MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 	
	@Mode = 8,
	@Id = @CompanyId,
	@CrmUserId = @UserId,
	@IsAccess = @IsAccessCompanyId OUTPUT
				
	IF(@IsAccessCompanyId = 0)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @FoundCmpName NVARCHAR(2000)
		SET @FoundCmpName = (SELECT
							CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
								THEN (SELECT  
										ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId),'') + ' ' +
										ISNULL(cmpName,'')
								FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @CompanyId)
								ELSE (SELECT  
										ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' +
										ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CL WHERE CL.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CL.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
										FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @CompanyId)
							END
							)
							
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=CompanyAccess|' + ISNULL(@FoundCmpName,'') + '|Msg=CompanyAccess|'
	END	 
END
/**/
IF(@cmpProvinceId IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @IsAccessProvinceId BIT = 0

	EXEC dbo.MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 	
	@Mode = 1,
	@Id = @cmpProvinceId,
	@CrmUserId = @UserId,
	@IsAccess = @IsAccessProvinceId OUTPUT
				
	IF(@IsAccessProvinceId = 0)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @ProvinceName NVARCHAR(1000)
		SET @ProvinceName = (SELECT
							CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
								THEN (SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province P WHERE P.ProvinceID = @cmpProvinceId)
								ELSE (SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province_Lang P WHERE P.ProvinceID = @cmpProvinceId AND P.LanguageID = @LanguageID)
							END
							)
					
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=CmpProvinceIdAccess|' + ISNULL(@ProvinceName,'') + '|Msg=CmpProvinceIdAccess|'
	END																																				 	 
END
/**/
IF(@cmpCityId IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @IsAccessCityId BIT = 0

	EXEC dbo.MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 	
	@Mode = 2,
	@Id = @cmpCityId,
	@CrmUserId = @UserId,
	@IsAccess = @IsAccessCityId OUTPUT
				
	IF(@IsAccessCityId = 0)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @CityName NVARCHAR(1000)
		SET @CityName = (SELECT
							CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
								THEN (SELECT City FROM dbo.City C WHERE C.CityID = @cmpCityId)
								ELSE (SELECT City FROM dbo.City_Lang C WHERE C.CityID = @cmpCityId AND C.LanguageID = @LanguageID)
							END
							)					

		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=CmpCityIdAccess|' + ISNULL(@CityName,'') + '|Msg=CmpCityIdAccess|'
	END																					 
END
/**/
/*==============================================================================*/
/*==============================================================================*/
/*==============================================================================*/
IF(@ErrorMessage = '' OR @ErrorMessage IS NULL) /*Is not Duplicate =====> update*/
BEGIN
	UPDATE [dbo].[CrmCompany] 
		SET
		/*AI990630 - pre tel added*/
		[cmpPreTel] = @cmpPreTel
		,[cmpTel] = @cmpTel
		,[cmpCountryId] = @cmpCountryId
		,[cmpProvinceId] = @cmpProvinceId
		,[cmpCityId] = @cmpCityId
		,[cmpAddress] = @cmpAddress
		,[UserId] = @UserId 
		,[Year1] = @Year1
		,[Month1] = @Month1
		,[RegisterDate] = @RegisterDate
		,[RegisterDatePersian] = @RegisterDatePersian
		,[AppType] = @AppType
		,[cmpCoordinateLatitude] = @cmpCoordinateLatitude
		,[cmpCoordinateLongitude] = @cmpCoordinateLongitude
		,[cmpCoordinateLastDate] = @cmpCoordinateLastDate
		,[cmpCoordinateLastDatePersian] = @cmpCoordinateLastDatePersian
		,[cmpCoordinateZoomLevel] = @cmpCoordinateZoomLevel
		,[cmpCoordinateLastUserId] = @cmpCoordinateLastUserId
	WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId
	/*********** Update to lang table ***********/
	UPDATE [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang]
	   SET 
		  [cmpAddress] = @cmpAddress
	 WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId
	 AND [LanguageID] IN (SELECT LN.LanguageID FROM dbo.Languages LN 
							WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
							AND LN.IsActive = 1)		
	/******* Insert to multi group table ********/
	IF(@cmpMultiGroups IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		/************ For add group *************/
		DECLARE @GroupId INT
		DECLARE CrsrMultiGroup CURSOR FOR
			SELECT splitdata 
			FROM dbo.fnSplitString(@cmpMultiGroups, '|')		
		/**/
		OPEN CrsrMultiGroup
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrMultiGroup INTO @GroupId
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
			/***** Find group multi for the company  *****/
			DECLARE @CompanyGroupsMultiId INT
			SET @CompanyGroupsMultiId = (SELECT TOP 1 CompanyGroupsMultiID FROM dbo.CrmCompanyMultiGroups
											WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId
											AND CompanyGroupsMultiID = CAST(@GroupId AS INT))
			/*IF multi group is not then add*/
			IF(@CompanyGroupsMultiId IS NULL)
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyMultiGroups]
				   (
					[CompanyID]
				   ,[CompanyGroupsMultiID]
				   ,[UserId]
				   ,[Year1]
				   ,[Month1]
				   ,[RegisterDate]
				   ,[RegisterDatePersian]
				   ,[AppType]
				   ,[LanguageID]
				   ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				   ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				   ,[FirstRegisterDate]
					)
				VALUES
				   (
					@CompanyId
				   ,CAST(@GroupId AS INT)
				   ,@UserId
				   ,@Year1
				   ,@Month1
				   ,@RegisterDate
				   ,@RegisterDatePersian
				   ,@AppType
				   ,@DefaultLanguageID
				   ,@UserId
				   ,@RegisterDatePersian
				   ,@RegisterDate
				   )
			END																							 					
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrMultiGroup INTO @GroupId
		END
		CLOSE CrsrMultiGroup
		DEALLOCATE CrsrMultiGroup
		/************ For delete group *************/
		DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyMultiGroups]
					WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyId
					AND CompanyGroupsMultiID NOT IN(SELECT CAST(splitdata AS INT) 
														FROM dbo.fnSplitString(@cmpMultiGroups, '|'))				
	END				 	 	 
END
/**/
IF (@@ERROR > 0)
BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Update Company' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
ELSE 
COMMIT TRAN 
/**/
SET @UpdatedCompanyId = @CompanyId
/**/
/**/
/**/
IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
BEGIN 
	RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
 	SELECT 0
END 
ELSE
BEGIN
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	SELECT 1
END	
/**/
END






















GO
-- [dbo].[MobspUpdateCompanyPerson] | References permission/temp-filter machinery





/*RZ961119*/
/*RZ961129- Multi language*/
/*RZ961207- Remove check for duplicate and check duplicatr just prsMoblie and prsEmails*/
/*RZ971113- Auto send SMS after save mobile*/
/*RZ971218- Ckeck is null prsFName*/
/*RZ971222- Add check access to cmp*/
/*AI990805 - dup checks added*/
/*AI990827 - remove = -1 checks and use = null for ints and =null & ='' for Strings - add cmpMainPerson, cmpIsdraft, cmpKind*/
/*AI14000304 - preTel + Tel for checking duplicate instead of Tel*/
/*AI14010329 - User Mobsp instead of CRMsp for dup checks*/
/*AI14010330 - add [CantSaveDupCmpMainPrsNationalCode] check*/
/*AI14010621 - Use System datetime for FirstRegisterDate And RegisterDate*/
/*AI14011027 - Convert Arabic characters to Persian*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspUpdateCompanyPerson]
/*
	EXEC MobspUpdateCompanyPerson	
*/
(
@PersonId INT,
@Person NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
@CompanyId INT,
@UserId INT,
@Year1 SMALLINT,
@AppType VARCHAR(20), 
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
/*RZ961207- Remove check for duplicate and check duplicatr just prsMoblie and prsEmails*/
@LackOfShowText NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
/**/
@UpdatedPersonId INT OUTPUT,
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** TRANSACTION *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/***************************** Define fields ************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
DECLARE @IsDuplicate BIT,
	@prsLName NVARCHAR(100),
	@prsFName NVARCHAR(50),
	@prsTitleId INT,
	@prsJobId INT,
	@prsTypeId INT,
	@prsGroupId INT,
	@prsSexId INT,
	@prsEducationId INT,
	@prsPreTel NVARCHAR(10),
	@prsTel NVARCHAR(15),
	@prsTelUpTo NVARCHAR(10),
	@prsTelDesc NVARCHAR(50),
	@prsTelII NVARCHAR(15),
	@prsFax NVARCHAR(15),
	@prsFaxDesc NVARCHAR(50),
	@prsMobile NVARCHAR(15),
	@prsWebSite NVARCHAR(50),
	@prsEmail NVARCHAR(50),
	@prsEmailII NVARCHAR(50),
	@prsCountryId INT,
	@prsProvinceId INT,
	@prsCityId INT,
	@prsAddress NVARCHAR(250),
	@prsAddressDesc NVARCHAR(100),
	@prsZipCode NVARCHAR(50),
	@prsPostBox NVARCHAR(50),
	@prsNationalCode NVARCHAR(30),
	@prsIdentityNumber NVARCHAR(30),
	@prsFatherName NVARCHAR(50),
	@prsBirthDate NVARCHAR(8),
	@prsBirthDateTime DATETIME,
	@prsBirthPlace NVARCHAR(50),
	@prsIsMarried BIT,
	@prsMarriageDate NVARCHAR(8),
	@prsMarriageDateTime DATETIME,
	@prsEducationField NVARCHAR(50),
	@prsSkill NVARCHAR(50),
	@prsStudyField NVARCHAR(50),
	@prsInteresting NVARCHAR(500),
	@prsSpecialPoint NVARCHAR(500),
	@prsDescription NVARCHAR(500),
	@Month1 SMALLINT,
	@RegisterDate DATETIME,
	@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
	@RegisterTime NVARCHAR(8),
	@prsDepartmentId INT,
	@prsStopSendSms BIT,
	@prsOrderView INT,
	@prsWorkingOut BIT
/************************************ Error *************************************/
DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT
/************************************ REGISTER DATE *************************************/ /*AI14010621*/
SET @RegisterDate = GETDATE()
SET @RegisterDatePersian = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](@RegisterDate))
SET @RegisterTime = (CONVERT(CHAR(8), @RegisterDate, 108))
SET @Month1 = CASE WHEN @RegisterDatePersian = NULL THEN NULL ELSE CAST(SUBSTRING(@RegisterDatePersian,5,2) AS SMALLINT) END
/************** Set CantSaveDupPrsMob & CantSaveDupPrsEmail *********************/
/*AI990805 - ,@CantSaveDupTel BIT, @CantSaveDupCmpFax BIT, @CantSaveDupCmpZipCode BIT, @CantSaveDupPrsNationalCode BIT added*/
DECLARE @CantSaveDupPrsMob BIT, @CantSaveDupPrsemail BIT,@CantSaveDupTel BIT, @CantSaveDupCmpFax BIT, @CantSaveDupCmpZipCode BIT, @CantSaveDupPrsNationalCode BIT,
	@CantSaveDupCmpMainPrsNationalCode BIT /*AI140103*/
SELECT TOP 1 
      @CantSaveDupPrsMob = [CantSaveDupPrsMob]
      , @CantSaveDupPrsEmail =[CantSaveDupPrsEmail]
		,@CantSaveDupTel = [CantSaveDupTel]
		,@CantSaveDupCmpFax = [CantSaveDupFax]
		,@CantSaveDupCmpZipCode = [CantSaveDupZipCode]
		,@CantSaveDupPrsNationalCode = [CantSaveDupPrsNationalCode]
		,@CantSaveDupCmpMainPrsNationalCode = [CantSaveDupCmpMainPrsNationalCode]
 FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting]
/********************************************************************************/
/***************************** Split fields *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
SET @IsDuplicate  = 0
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Person ******************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRAN
/**/
DECLARE @FindRecord INT
SET @FindRecord = (SELECT TOP 1 P.CompanyPersonID FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons P WHERE P.CompanyPersonID = @PersonId) 
/**/
IF(@Person IS NOT NULL AND @Person <> '' AND @PersonId > 0 AND @FindRecord > 0 AND @FindRecord IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
DECLARE @PrsField NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
WHILE LEN(@Person) > 0
BEGIN
    IF PATINDEX('%|#|%', @Person) > 0
    BEGIN
        SET @PrsField = SUBSTRING(@Person,
                                    0,
                                    PATINDEX('%|#|%', @Person))
        /*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill variabele ------------------------------*/
        /*Get parameter name and split value form parameter*/
		/*For example => prsTypeId=102 => prsTypeId= => prsTypeId => Compare => value*/
		DECLARE @PrsFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
		DECLARE @PrsFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
		SET @PrsFieldName = SUBSTRING(@PrsField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@PrsField)-1)                                    
		SET @PrsFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@PrsField, CHARINDEX('=', @PrsField)+1, Len(@PrsField))
		/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
		/*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill variabele ------------------------------*/
		/*BEGIN PERSON*/
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsLName'
		BEGIN
			SET @prsLName = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END						
			SET @prsLName = (SELECT dbo.[fnConvertArabicCharacterToPersian](@prsLName)) /*AI14011027*/
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsFName'
		BEGIN
			SET @prsFName = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
			SET @prsFName = (SELECT dbo.[fnConvertArabicCharacterToPersian](@prsFName)) /*AI14011027*/
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsTitleId'
		BEGIN
			SELECT @prsTitleId = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsJobId'
		BEGIN
			SELECT @prsJobId = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsTypeId'
		BEGIN
			SELECT @prsTypeId = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsGroupId'
		BEGIN
			SELECT @prsGroupId = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsSexId'
		BEGIN
			SELECT @prsSexId = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsEducationId'
		BEGIN
			SELECT @prsEducationId = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsPreTel'
		BEGIN
			SET @prsPreTel = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsTel'
		BEGIN
			SET @prsTel = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
			/*RZ961207- Remove check for duplicate and check duplicatr just prsMoblie and prsEmails*/
			/*IF(@prsTel IS NOT NULL)--check duplicate
			BEGIN					
				DECLARE	@ResultCheckDuplicateForPrsTel INT = 0	
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCompany]
						@Type  = 0, 
						@Id = @CompanyId, 
						@Val = @prsTel , 
						@TitleID = 0, 
						@CityId = 0, 
						@UserID  = @UserId ,							
						@Result = @ResultCheckDuplicateForPrsTel OUTPUT,
						@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
						@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

				IF(@ResultCheckDuplicateForPrsTel > 0)
					SET @IsDuplicate = 1						
			END	*/																			
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsTelUpTo'
		BEGIN
			SET @prsTelUpTo = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsTelDesc'
		BEGIN
			SET @prsTelDesc = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsTelII'
		BEGIN
			SET @prsTelII = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
			/*RZ961207- Remove check for duplicate and check duplicatr just prsMoblie and prsEmails*/
			/*IF(@prsTelII IS NOT NULL)--check duplicate
			BEGIN					
				DECLARE	@ResultCheckDuplicateForPrsTelII INT = 0	
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCompany]
						@Type  = 0, 
						@Id = @CompanyId, 
						@Val = @prsTelII , 
						@TitleID = 0, 
						@CityId = 0, 
						@UserID  = @UserId ,							
						@Result = @ResultCheckDuplicateForPrsTelII OUTPUT,
						/*RZ961129- Multi language*/
						@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
						@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID
				IF(@ResultCheckDuplicateForPrsTelII > 0)
					SET @IsDuplicate = 1						
			END*/
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsFax'
		BEGIN
			SET @prsFax = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
			/*RZ961207- Remove check for duplicate and check duplicatr just prsMoblie and prsEmails*/
			/*IF(@prsFax IS NOT NULL)--check duplicate
			BEGIN					
				DECLARE	@ResultCheckDuplicateForPrsFax INT = 0	
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCompany]
						@Type  = 0, 
						@Id = @CompanyId, 
						@Val = @prsFax ,

		END

        END AI14010621 */
		/*--------------------------*/		
		/*END PERSON*/						
        /*END GENERAL*/
        /*---------------------------- END Fill variabele --------------------------------*/
        /*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/
        SET @Person = SUBSTRING(@Person,
                                  LEN(@PrsField + '|#|') + 1,
                                  LEN(@Person))
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SET @PrsField = @Person
        SET @Person = NULL        
    END

/**/
IF(@CompanyId IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @IsAccessCompanyId BIT = 0

	EXEC dbo.MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 	
	@Mode = 8,
	@Id = @CompanyId,
	@CrmUserId = @UserId,
	@IsAccess = @IsAccessCompanyId OUTPUT
				
	IF(@IsAccessCompanyId = 0)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @FoundCmpName NVARCHAR(2000)
		SET @FoundCmpName = (SELECT
							CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
								THEN (SELECT  
										ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId),'') + ' ' +
										ISNULL(cmpName,'')
								FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @CompanyId)
								ELSE (SELECT  
										ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' +
										ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CL WHERE CL.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CL.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
										FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @CompanyId)
							END
							)
									
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=CompanyAccess|' + CAST(@PersonId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '|PRSID|' + ISNULL(@FoundCmpName,'') + '|Msg=CompanyAccess|'
	END	 
END

/*RZ961207- Remove check for duplicate and check duplicatr just prsMoblie and prsEmails*/
/******************************* prsMobile *********************************************/
IF(@ErrorMessage = '' OR @ErrorMessage IS NULL)
BEGIN
	IF(@prsMobile IS NOT NULL AND @prsMobile <> '' AND @CantSaveDupPrsMob = 1)
	BEGIN			
	DECLARE @HavePermissionDuplicatePrsMobile INT
	IF EXISTS (
	SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
	WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' )
	AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actCanSaveMobDupAtTimeLimit'
						AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' ))
	AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
	SET @HavePermissionDuplicatePrsMobile = 1

	ELSE
	SET @HavePermissionDuplicatePrsMobile = 0

	IF(@HavePermissionDuplicatePrsMobile = 0 AND @CantSaveDupPrsMob = 1)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @ResultDupcmpNumber NVARCHAR(50) = ''
		EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicatePrsMobile]
			@prsMobile = @prsMobile, 
			@CurrId = @PersonId,
			@ResultDupcmpNumber = @ResultDupcmpNumber OUTPUT 		
		IF(@ResultDupcmpNumber IS NOT NULL AND @ResultDupcmpNumber <> '')
		BEGIN
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '@prsMobile=Dup,@prsLName='+ ISNULL(@prsLName,'') + ',@prsFName='+ ISNULL(@prsFName,'') +
			 ',@CompanyPersonID=' + ISNULL(CAST(@PersonId AS NVARCHAR(10)),'') + 
			 ',@prsCompanyId=' + ISNULL(CAST(@CompanyId AS NVARCHAR(10)),'') 
			 + ',@DupCmpNumber=' + ISNULL(@ResultDupcmpNumber,'') + '|^|'	
		END	
	END


											
END
/******************************* prsEmail **********************************************/
IF(@prsEmail IS NOT NULL AND @prsEmail <> '' AND @CantSaveDupPrsEmail = 1)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @ResultDupcmpNumberForPrsEmail NVARCHAR(50) = ''
	EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicatePrsEmail]
	@prsEmail = @prsEmail, 
	@CurrId = @PersonId,
	@ResultDupcmpNumber = @ResultDupcmpNumberForPrsEmail OUTPUT 	
	IF(@ResultDupcmpNumberForPrsEmail IS NOT NULL AND @ResultDupcmpNumberForPrsEmail <> '')
	BEGIN
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '@prsEmail=Dup,@prsLName='+ ISNULL(@prsLName,'') + ',@prsFName='+ ISNULL(@prsFName,'') +/*RZ971218- Ckeck is null prsFName*/
		 ',@CompanyPersonID=' + ISNULL(CAST(@PersonId AS NVARCHAR(10)),'') + 
		 ',@prsCompanyId=' + ISNULL(CAST(@CompanyId AS NVARCHAR(10)),'') + ',@DupCmpNumber=' + ISNULL(@ResultDupcmpNumberForPrsEmail,'') + '|^|'	
	END											
END

/*AI990805 - below checks added*/
DECLARE @DupCompanyId INT , @DupCmpTitle NVARCHAR(MAX), @DupCmpName NVARCHAR(MAX), @DupCmpCity NVARCHAR(MAX), @DupCmpNumber INT
/******************************* prsTel *********************************************/
IF(@prsTel IS NOT NULL AND @prsTel <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	DECLARE @HavePermissionDuplicatePrsTel INT
	IF EXISTS (
	SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
	WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' )
	AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actCanSaveTelDupAtTimeLimit'
						AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' ))
	AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
	SET @HavePermissionDuplicatePrsTel = 1

	ELSE
	SET @HavePermissionDuplicatePrsTel = 0

	IF(@HavePermissionDuplicatePrsTel = 0 AND @CantSaveDupTel = 1)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		DECLARE @TmpTableForCheckForDuplicate TABLE (
		[CompanyID] INT,	
		[cmpNumber] INT,	
		[CompanyName] NVARCHAR(MAX),
		[CompanyPersonId] INT,	
		[PersonName] NVARCHAR(MAX))

		/*AI14000304 - preTel + Tel for checking duplicate instead of Tel*/
		DECLARE @chckPrsTel NVARCHAR(100)
		SET @chckPrsTel = ISNULL(@prsPreTel, '') + @prsTel
				
		INSERT INTO @TmpTableForCheckForDuplicate
		EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateTel] /*AI14010329*/
				@Tel  = @chckPrsTel ,
				@Mode = 2, 
				@CurrId = @PersonId, 
				@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
				@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID
		SELECT TOP 1
			@DupCompanyId = [CompanyID], 
			@DupCmpName = [CompanyName], 
			@DupCmpNumber = [cmpNumber]
		FROM @TmpTableForCheckForDuplicate
		
		IF(@DupCompanyId IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '@prsTel=Dup,' + '@DupCmpName=' + ISNULL(@DupCmpName,'') +
			',@DupCmpNumber=' + ISNULL(CAST(@DupCmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(10)),'') + '|^|'	
		END
	/**/				
	END
/**/
END
/*---------------------- SET NULL ---------------*/
SET @DupCompanyId = NULL SET @DupCmpTitle = NULL SET @DupCmpName = NULL SET @DupCmpCity = NULL SET @DupCmpNumber = NULL
DELETE FROM @TmpTableForCheckForDuplicate

		BEGIN
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '@prsZipCode=Dup,' + '@DupCmpName=' + ISNULL(@DupCmpName,'') +
			',@DupCmpNumber=' + ISNULL(CAST(@DupCmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(10)),'') + '|^|'	
		END
	/**/				
	END
/**/
END
/*---------------------- SET NULL ---------------*/
SET @DupCompanyId = NULL SET @DupCmpTitle = NULL SET @DupCmpName = NULL SET @DupCmpCity = NULL SET @DupCmpNumber = NULL
DELETE FROM @TmpTableForCheckForDuplicate
/******************************* cmpNationalCode *********************************************/
IF(@prsNationalCode IS NOT NULL AND @prsNationalCode <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(@CantSaveDupPrsNationalCode= 1)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		INSERT INTO @TmpTableForCheckForDuplicate 
		EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicatePrsNationalCode] /*AI14010329*/
				@PrsNationalCode  = @prsNationalCode ,
				@CurrId = @PersonId, 
				@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
				@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

		SELECT TOP 1
			@DupCompanyId = [CompanyID], 
			@DupCmpName = [CompanyName], 
			@DupCmpNumber = [cmpNumber]
		FROM @TmpTableForCheckForDuplicate	 
		
		IF(@DupCompanyId IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '@prsNationalCode=Dup,' + '@DupCmpName=' + ISNULL(@DupCmpName,'') +
			',@DupCmpNumber=' + ISNULL(CAST(@DupCmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(10)),'') + '|^|'	
		END
	/**/				
	END
	ELSE IF(@CantSaveDupCmpMainPrsNationalCode = 1) /*AI14010330*/
	BEGIN
		INSERT INTO @TmpTableForCheckForDuplicate 
		EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCmpMainPrsNationalCodeByCmpKind]
				@PrsNationalCode  = @prsNationalCode ,
				@CurrId = @PersonId,
				@CmpKind = 1,
				@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
				@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

		SELECT TOP 1
			@DupCompanyId = [CompanyID], 
			@DupCmpName = [CompanyName], 
			@DupCmpNumber = [cmpNumber]
		FROM @TmpTableForCheckForDuplicate	 
		
		IF(@DupCompanyId IS NOT NULL)
		BEGIN
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '@mainPrsNationalCode=Dup,' + '@DupCmpName=' + ISNULL(@DupCmpName,'') +
			',@DupCmpNumber=' + ISNULL(CAST(@DupCmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(10)),'') + '|^|'	
		END
	END
/**/
END
/*---------------------- SET NULL ---------------*/
SET @DupCompanyId = NULL SET @DupCmpTitle = NULL SET @DupCmpName = NULL SET @DupCmpCity = NULL SET @DupCmpNumber = NULL
DELETE FROM @TmpTableForCheckForDuplicate
/*AI990805 - until here*/
/*==============================================================================*/
IF(@ErrorMessage = '' OR @ErrorMessage IS NULL) /*Is not Duplicate =====> update*/
BEGIN
	DECLARE @FindCompanyId INT
	SET @FindCompanyId = (SELECT TOP 1 CompanyID FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @CompanyId)
	IF(@FindCompanyId IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		/*RZ971113- Auto send SMS after save mobile*/
		DECLARE @prsMobileBeforeEdit NVARCHAR(50)
		SET @prsMobileBeforeEdit = (SELECT TOP 1 prsMobile FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] WHERE CompanyPersonID = @PersonId)		
		/*RZ971113- END*/
		/******* Update to table *************/
		UPDATE [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons]
		   SET 
			   [prsLName] = @prsLName
			  ,[prsFName] = @prsFName
			  ,[prsTitleId] = @prsTitleId
			  ,[prsJobId] = @prsJobId
			  ,[prsTypeId] = @prsTypeId
			  ,[prsGroupId] = @prsGroupId
			  ,[prsSexId] = @prsSexId
			  ,[prsEducationId] = @prsEducationId
			  ,[prsPreTel] = @prsPreTel
			  ,[prsTel] = @prsTel
			  ,[prsTelUpTo] = @prsTelUpTo
			  ,[prsTelDesc] = @prsTelDesc
			  ,[prsTelII] = @prsTelII
			  ,[prsFax] = @prsFax
			  ,[prsFaxDesc] = @prsFaxDesc
			  ,[prsMobile] = @prsMobile
			  ,[prsWebSite] = @prsWebSite
			  ,[prsEmail] = @prsEmail
			  ,[prsEmailII] = @prsEmailII
			  ,[prsCountryId] = @prsCountryId
			  ,[prsProvinceId] = @prsProvinceId
			  ,[prsCityId] = @prsCityId
			  ,[prsAddress] = @prsAddress
			  ,[prsAddressDesc] = @prsAddressDesc
			  ,[prsZipCode] = @prsZipCode
			  ,[prsPostBox] = @prsPostBox
			  ,[prsNationalCode] = @prsNationalCode
			  ,[prsIdentityNumber] = @prsIdentityNumber
			  ,[prsFatherName] = @prsFatherName
			  ,[prsBirthDate] = @prsBirthDate
			  ,[prsBirthDateTime] = @prsBirthDateTime
			  ,[prsBirthPlace] = @prsBirthPlace
			  ,[prsIsMarried] = @prsIsMarried
			  ,[prsMarriageDate] = @prsMarriageDate
			  ,[prsMarriageDateTime] = @prsMarriageDateTime
			  ,[prsEducationField] = @prsEducationField
			  ,[prsSkill] = @prsSkill
			  ,[prsStudyField] = @prsStudyField
			  ,[prsInteresting] = @prsInteresting
			  ,[prsSpecialPoint] = @prsSpecialPoint
			  ,[prsDescription] = @prsDescription
			  ,[UserId] = @UserId
			  ,[Year1] = @Year1
			  ,[Month1] = @Month1
			  ,[RegisterDate] = @RegisterDate
			  ,[RegisterDatePersian] = @RegisterDatePersian
			  ,[prsDepartmentId] = @prsDepartmentId
			  ,[prsStopSendSms] = @prsStopSendSms
			  ,[prsOrderView] = @prsOrderView
			  ,[prsWorkingOut] = @prsWorkingOut				  				  
			  ,[AppType] = @AppType				  				  
		 WHERE CompanyPersonID = @PersonId
		/******* Update to lang table ********/
		UPDATE [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang]
		   SET 
			   [prsLName] = @prsLName
			  ,[prsFName] = @prsFName
			  ,[prsTelDesc] = @prsTelDesc
			  ,[prsFaxDesc] = @prsFaxDesc
			  ,[prsAddress] = @prsAddress
			  ,[prsAddressDesc] = @prsAddressDesc
			  ,[prsFatherName] = @prsFatherName
			  ,[prsBirthPlace] = @prsBirthPlace
			  ,[prsEducationField] = @prsEducationField
			  ,[prsSkill] = @prsSkill
			  ,[prsStudyField] = @prsStudyField
			  ,[prsInteresting] = @prsInteresting
			  ,[prsSpecialPoint] = @prsSpecialPoint
			  ,[prsDescription] = @prsDescription
		 WHERE [CompanyPersonID] = @PersonId 
		 AND [LanguageID] IN (SELECT LN.LanguageID FROM dbo.Languages LN 
						WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
						AND LN.IsActive = 1)	
		/** Auto send SMS after save mobile **/
		/*RZ971113- Auto send SMS after save mobile*/
		IF(@prsMobile IS NOT NULL AND @prsMobile <> '')
		BEGIN
			IF((@prsMobileBeforeEdit IS NULL OR @prsMobileBeforeEdit = '') OR (@prsMobileBeforeEdit <> @prsMobile))
			BEGIN
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspAutoSendSMSAfterSaveMobile]
					@CompanyId =  @CompanyId,
					@CompanyPersonId = @PersonId,
					@MobileNumber = @prsMobile,
					@UserId = @UserId,
					@AppType =@AppType,
					@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
					@SendNeedCheck =  0
			END
		END										
		/*RZ971113- END*/													
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage  + '|Msg=0'
	END
END
/**/
IF (@@ERROR > 0)
BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Update Person' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
ELSE 
COMMIT TRAN 
/**/
/**/
SET @UpdatedPersonId = @PersonId
/**/
IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
BEGIN 
	RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
 	SELECT 0
END 
ELSE
BEGIN
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	SELECT 1
END	
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[MobspUpdateCompanyPersonForPs] | References permission/temp-filter machinery





/*AI990321*/
/*این اس پی قرار است پاک شود و ای پی ای ادیت شود و یک اس پی جدید نوشته شود که برای تمام جاها استفاده شود*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspUpdateCompanyPersonForPs]
/*
	exec MobspUpdateCompanyPerson	
*/
(
@PersonId INT,
@Person NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
@CompanyId INT,
@UserId INT,
@Year1 SMALLINT,
@AppType VARCHAR(20), 
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
@LackOfShowText NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
/**/
@UpdatedPersonId INT OUTPUT,
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** TRANSACTION *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/***************************** Define fields ************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
DECLARE @IsDuplicate BIT,
	@prsLName NVARCHAR(100),
	@Month1 SMALLINT,
	@RegisterDate DATETIME,
	@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
	@RegisterTime NVARCHAR(8)
/************************************ Error *************************************/
DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT
/************** Set CantSaveDupPrsMob & CantSaveDupPrsEmail *********************/
DECLARE @CantSaveDupPrsMob BIT, @CantSaveDupPrsemail BIT
SELECT TOP 1 
      @CantSaveDupPrsMob = [CantSaveDupPrsMob]
      , @CantSaveDupPrsEmail =[CantSaveDupPrsEmail]      
 FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting]
/********************************************************************************/
/***************************** Split fields *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
SET @IsDuplicate  = 0
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Person ******************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRAN
/**/
DECLARE @FindRecord INT
SET @FindRecord = (SELECT TOP 1 P.CompanyPersonID FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons P WHERE P.CompanyPersonID = @PersonId) 
/**/
IF(@Person IS NOT NULL AND @Person <> '' AND @PersonId > 0 AND @FindRecord > 0 AND @FindRecord IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
DECLARE @PrsField NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
WHILE LEN(@Person) > 0
BEGIN
    IF PATINDEX('%|#|%', @Person) > 0
    BEGIN
        SET @PrsField = SUBSTRING(@Person,
                                    0,
                                    PATINDEX('%|#|%', @Person))
        /*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill variabele ------------------------------*/
        /*Get parameter name and split value form parameter*/
		/*For example => prsTypeId=102 => prsTypeId= => prsTypeId => Compare => value*/
		DECLARE @PrsFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
		DECLARE @PrsFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
		SET @PrsFieldName = SUBSTRING(@PrsField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@PrsField)-1)                                    
		SET @PrsFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@PrsField, CHARINDEX('=', @PrsField)+1, Len(@PrsField))
		/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
		/*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill variabele ------------------------------*/
		/*BEGIN PERSON*/
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsLName'
		BEGIN
			SET @prsLName = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END						
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @PrsFieldName = 'RegisterDatePersian'
        BEGIN
			SET @RegisterDatePersian = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE REPLACE(@PrsFieldValue, '/', '') END							
			SET @RegisterDate = CASE WHEN @RegisterDatePersian IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@RegisterDatePersian, null) END	
			SET @Month1 = CASE WHEN @RegisterDatePersian = NULL THEN NULL ELSE CAST(SUBSTRING(@RegisterDatePersian,5,2) AS SMALLINT) END											 	
        END 
		/*--------------------------*/		
		IF @PrsFieldName = 'RegisterTime'
        BEGIN
			SET @RegisterTime = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END										
        END
		/*--------------------------*/		
		/*END PERSON*/						
        /*END GENERAL*/
        /*---------------------------- END Fill variabele --------------------------------*/
        /*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/
        SET @Person = SUBSTRING(@Person,
                                  LEN(@PrsField + '|#|') + 1,
                                  LEN(@Person))
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SET @PrsField = @Person
        SET @Person = NULL        
    END
END
/************************** Set time ********************************************/
IF(@RegisterTime IS NOT NULL AND @RegisterDatePersian IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	SET @RegisterDate = CASE WHEN @RegisterDatePersian IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@RegisterDatePersian, @RegisterTime) END			 	
END
/*****/
/*************************************************************/
END
ELSE
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=2' 		

/**/
IF (@@ERROR > 0)
BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Input Person' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
ELSE 
COMMIT TRAN 
/**/
/*==============================================================================*/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Update Peson ************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRAN
/**/
/******************************* CHECK Access comapny and datapermission *************************/
/*RZ971222- Add check access to cmp and datapermission*/
/**/
IF(@CompanyId IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @IsAccessCompanyId BIT = 0

	EXEC dbo.MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 	
	@Mode = 8,
	@Id = @CompanyId,
	@CrmUserId = @UserId,
	@IsAccess = @IsAccessCompanyId OUTPUT
				
	IF(@IsAccessCompanyId = 0)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @FoundCmpName NVARCHAR(2000)
		SET @FoundCmpName = (SELECT
							CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
								THEN (SELECT  
										ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId),'') + ' ' +
										ISNULL(cmpName,'')
								FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @CompanyId)
								ELSE (SELECT  
										ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' +
										ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CL WHERE CL.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CL.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
										FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @CompanyId)
							END
							)
									
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=CompanyAccess|' + CAST(@PersonId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '|PRSID|' + ISNULL(@FoundCmpName,'') + '|Msg=CompanyAccess|'
	END	 
END
/**/
/*RZ971222- END*/
/*==============================================================================*/
/*==============================================================================*/
IF(@ErrorMessage = '' OR @ErrorMessage IS NULL) /*Is not Duplicate =====> update*/
BEGIN
	DECLARE @FindCompanyId INT
	SET @FindCompanyId = (SELECT TOP 1 CompanyID FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @CompanyId)
	IF(@FindCompanyId IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		/*RZ971113- Auto send SMS after save mobile*/
		DECLARE @prsMobileBeforeEdit NVARCHAR(50)
		SET @prsMobileBeforeEdit = (SELECT TOP 1 prsMobile FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] WHERE CompanyPersonID = @PersonId)		
		/*RZ971113- END*/
		/******* Update to table *************/
		UPDATE [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons]
		   SET 
			   [prsLName] = @prsLName
			  ,[UserId] = @UserId
			  ,[Year1] = @Year1
			  ,[Month1] = @Month1
			  ,[RegisterDate] = @RegisterDate
			  ,[RegisterDatePersian] = @RegisterDatePersian			  				  
			  ,[AppType] = @AppType				  				  
		 WHERE CompanyPersonID = @PersonId
		/******* Update to lang table ********/
		UPDATE [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang]
		   SET 
			   [prsLName] = @prsLName
		 WHERE [CompanyPersonID] = @PersonId 
		 AND [LanguageID] IN (SELECT LN.LanguageID FROM dbo.Languages LN 
						WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
						AND LN.IsActive = 1)	
		/** Auto send SMS after save mobile **/								
		/*RZ971113- END*/													
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage  + '|Msg=0'
	END
END
/**/
IF (@@ERROR > 0)
BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Update Person' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
ELSE 
COMMIT TRAN 
/**/
/**/
SET @UpdatedPersonId = @PersonId
/**/
IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
BEGIN 
	RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
 	SELECT 0
END 
ELSE
BEGIN
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	SELECT 1
END	
/**/
END




GO
-- [dbo].[MobspUpdateCompanyProject] | References permission/temp-filter machinery






/*RZ961119*/
/*RZ961216- Delete Time from prjRemindDateTime*/
/*RZ971103- Add field*/
/*RZ971222- Add check access to cmp and project*/
/*AI990827 - remove = -1 checks and use = null for ints and =null & ='' for Strings - add cmpMainPerson, cmpIsdraft, cmpKind*/
/*AI14000131 - Added fields from [prjHaveSms] up to [prjNumR]*/
/*AI14000201 - Add duplicate checks*/
/*AI14010317 - Add field*/
/*AI14010407 - Close Ticket*/
/*AI14010408 - Update cnvs based on prjIsTicekt*/
/*AI14010412 - Add feild to prj - avoid insert prjToDoDateTime */
/*AI14010416 - Add field*/
/*AI14010621 - Use System datetime for FirstRegisterDate And RegisterDate*/
/*AI14010728 - Add field*/
/*AI14020504 - Add field*/
/*AI14020718 - Add field - Add check*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspUpdateCompanyProject]
/*
	EXEC MobspUpdateCompanyProject  		
*/
(
@CompanyId INT,
@ProjectId INT,
@Project NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
@UserId INT,
@Year1 SMALLINT,
@AppType VARCHAR(20), 
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
/**/
@UpdatedProjectId NVARCHAR(1000) OUTPUT,
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** TRANSACTION *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/***************************** Define fields ************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
DECLARE @prjTitle NVARCHAR(100) ,
	@prjDesc NVARCHAR(300) ,
	@prjStartDate NVARCHAR(8) ,
	@prjStartDay NVARCHAR(20) ,
	@prjStartTime NVARCHAR(15) ,
	@prjStartDateTime DATETIME ,
	@prjStarterUserId INT ,
	@prjFinishedStatusId INT ,
	@prjIsFinished BIT ,
	@prjFinishedDate NVARCHAR(8) ,
	@prjFinishedDay NVARCHAR(50) ,
	@prjFinishedTime NVARCHAR(15) ,
	@prjFinishedDateTime DATETIME ,
	@prjFinishedUserId INT ,
	@prjGradeId INT ,
	@prjPriorityId INT ,
	@Month1 SMALLINT,
	@RegisterDate DATETIME,
	@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
	@RegisterTime NVARCHAR(8),
	@prjGroupId INT ,
	@prjcnvsStatusGroupId INT ,
	@prjOrderView INT ,
	@prjPostFix NVARCHAR(200) ,
	@prjRemindDate VARCHAR(8) ,
	@prjRemindDateTime DATETIME ,
	@prjInsertForbidden BIT ,
	@CurrYear SMALLINT,
	/*RZ971103- Add field*/ 	
	@prjFOCmpCanSee BIT,
	@prjIsTicket BIT,
	@prjUserDepartmentId INT,
	/*RZ971103- END*/	
	/*AI14000131 - Added*/
	@prjHaveSms BIT = NULL,
	@prjSmsPrsIds NVARCHAR(3000) = NULL,
	@prjSmsText NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
	@prjSmsSendDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@prjSmsSendDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@prjInquiryNum NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
	@prjSubject NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
	@prjTitleGroup NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
	@prjCategoryId INT = NULL,
	@prjDescI NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@prjNumRef NVARCHAR(150) = NULL,
	@prjNumR BIGINT = NULL,
	/*AI14000131 End*/
	/*AI14010317*/
	@prjToDoDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@prjToDoTime NVARCHaR(15) = NULL,
	@prjToDoDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
	@prjToDoRegisterDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@prjToDoRegisterTime NVARCHaR(15) = NULL,
	@prjToDoRegisterDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
	@prjToDoDoneDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@prjToDoDoneTime NVARCHaR(15) = NULL,
	@prjToDoDoneDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
	@prjFOCmpCanInsCnvs BIT = NULL,
	@prjFOCmpCanNotInsCnvs BIT = NULL,
	@prjTicketStop BIT = NULL,
	@prjIsTicketNew BIT = NULL,
	/*AI14010317-end*/
	@prjToDoTimeSec BIGINT = NULL, /*AI14010412*/
	@prjToDoTimeRegisterHistory NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL, /*AI14010416*/
	/*AI14010728*/
	@prjcmpPresentationId INT = NULL,
	@prjcmpCampaignIds NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
	/*AI14010728-End*/
	@prjcmpQuickRegisterDefSettingId INT = NULL, /*AI14020504*/
	/*AI14020718*/
	@prjValidityPrdId INT,
	@prjValidityOrdItemId INT
	/*AI14020718-End*/
/************************************ Error *************************************/
DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT, @IsDuplicate BIT /*AI14000201*/
/************************************ REGISTER DATE *************************************/ /*AI14010621*/
SET @RegisterDate = GETDATE()
SET @RegisterDatePersian = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](@RegisterDate))
SET @RegisterTime = (CONVERT(CHAR(8), @RegisterDate, 108))
SET @Month1 = CASE WHEN @RegisterDatePersian = NULL THEN NULL ELSE CAST(SUBSTRING(@RegisterDatePersian,5,2) AS SMALLINT) END
SET @CurrYear = CASE WHEN @RegisterDatePersian = NULL THEN NULL ELSE CAST(SUBSTRING(@RegisterDatePersian, 1, 4) AS SMALLINT) END
/********************************************************************************/
/***************************** Split fields *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Project *****************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRAN
/**/
/**/
DECLARE @FindRecord INT
SET @FindRecord = (SELECT TOP 1 P.ProjectID FROM dbo.CrmProjects P WHERE P.ProjectID = @ProjectId) 
/**/
IF(@Project IS NOT NULL AND @Project <> '' AND @ProjectId > 0 AND @FindRecord > 0 AND @FindRecord IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
DECLARE @PrjField NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
WHILE LEN(@Project) > 0
BEGIN
    IF PATINDEX('%|#|%', @Project) > 0
    BEGIN
        SET @PrjField = SUBSTRING(@Project,
                                    0,
                                    PATINDEX('%|#|%', @Project))
        /*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill variabele ------------------------------*/
        /*Get parameter name and split value form parameter*/
		/*For example => prjGroupId=102 => prjGroupId= => prjGroupId => Compare => value*/
		DECLARE @PrjFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
		DECLARE @PrjFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
		SET @PrjFieldName = SUBSTRING(@PrjField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@PrjField)-1)                                    
		SET @PrjFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@PrjField, CHARINDEX('=', @PrjField)+1, Len(@PrjField))
		/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
		/*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill variabele ------------------------------*/
		/*BEGIN PROJECT*/
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @PrjFieldName = 'prjTitle'
		BEGIN
			SET @prjTitle = CASE WHEN @PrjFieldValue = '' OR @PrjFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrjFieldValue END	
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @PrjFieldName = 'prjDesc'
		BEGIN
			SET @prjDesc = CASE WHEN @PrjFieldValue = '' OR @PrjFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrjFieldValue END	
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @PrjFieldName = 'prjStartDate'
		BEGIN
			SET @prjStartDate = CASE WHEN @PrjFieldValue = '' OR @PrjFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE REPLACE(@PrjFieldValue, '/', '') END							
			SET @prjStartDateTime = CASE WHEN @prjStartDate IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@prjStartDate, null) END			 	
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @PrjFieldName = 'prjStartDay'
		BEGIN
			SET @prjStartDay = CASE WHEN @PrjFieldValue = '' OR @PrjFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrjFieldValue END	
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @PrjFieldName = 'prjStartTime'
		BEGIN
			SET @prjStartTime = CASE WHEN @PrjFieldValue = '' OR @PrjFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrjFieldValue END	
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @PrjFieldName = 'prjStarterUserId'
		BEGIN
			SELECT @prjStarterUserId = CASE WHEN @PrjFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrjFieldValue AS INT) END
		END
		/*--------------------------*/
		IF @PrjFieldName = 'prjFinishedStatusId'
		BEGIN

        /*END GENERAL*/

        SET @Project = SUBSTRING(@Project,

/*AI14000201 - Add duplicate checks*/
/**************NumRef Check Dup****************/
IF(ISNULL(@prjNumRef, '') <> '')
BEGIN
	IF EXISTS (
	SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
	WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmDefineProjects' )
	AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actNotDupNumRef'
						AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmDefineProjects' ))
	AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
	BEGIN
		EXEC [MobspCheckDuplicatePrjNumRef]
			@prjNumRef = @prjNumRef,
			@CurrId = @ProjectId,
			@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
			@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

		IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
		BEGIN
			SET @IsDuplicate = 1
		END
	END
END
/**************PrjDesc Check Dup****************/
IF(ISNULL(@PrjDesc, '') <> '')
BEGIN
	IF EXISTS (
	SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
	WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmDefineProjects' )
	AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actNotCanSavePrjDescDup'
						AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmDefineProjects' ))
	AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
	BEGIN
		EXEC [MobspCheckDuplicatePrjDesc]
			@PrjDesc = @PrjDesc,
			@CurrId = @ProjectId,
			@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
			@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

		IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
		BEGIN
			SET @IsDuplicate = 1
		END
	END
END
/**************PostFix Check Dup****************/
IF(ISNULL(@PrjPostFix, '') <> '')
BEGIN
	IF EXISTS (
	SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
	WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmDefineProjects' )
	AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actNotCanSavePrjPostFixDup'
						AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmDefineProjects' ))
	AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
	BEGIN
		EXEC [MobspCheckDuplicatePrjPostFix]
			@PrjPostFix = @PrjPostFix,
			@CurrId = @ProjectId,
			@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
			@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

		IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
		BEGIN
			SET @IsDuplicate = 1
		END
	END
END
/**************InquiryNum Check Dup****************/
IF(ISNULL(@prjInquiryNum, '') <> '')
BEGIN
	IF EXISTS (
	SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
	WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmDefineProjects' )
	AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actNotDupInquiryNumByCmpPrj'
						AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmDefineProjects' ))
	AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
	BEGIN
		EXEC [MobspCheckDuplicatePrjInquiryNum]
			@CompanyId = @CompanyId,
			@prjInquiryNum = @prjInquiryNum,
			@CurrId = @ProjectId,
			@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
			@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

		IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
		BEGIN
			SET @IsDuplicate = 1
		END
	END
	ELSE IF EXISTS (
	SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
	WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmDefineProjects' )
	AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actNotDupInquiryNumByAllPrj'
						AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmDefineProjects' ))
	AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
	BEGIN
		EXEC [MobspCheckDuplicatePrjInquiryNum]
			@CompanyId = 0,
			@prjInquiryNum = @prjInquiryNum,
			@CurrId = @ProjectId,
			@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
			@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

		IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
		BEGIN
			SET @IsDuplicate = 1
		END
	END
END
/**************NumR Check Dup****************/
IF(ISNULL(@prjNumR, 0) <> 0)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @CantSaveDupPrjNumRByCmpPrj BIT
	DECLARE @CantSaveDupPrjNumRByAllPrj BIT

	SELECT TOP 1 
		@CantSaveDupPrjNumRByCmpPrj = [IsActivePrjNumRByCmpPrj]
		,@CantSaveDupPrjNumRByAllPrj = [IsActivePrjNumRByAllPrj]
	FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting]

	IF(@CantSaveDupPrjNumRByCmpPrj = 1)
	BEGIN
		EXEC [MobspCheckDuplicatePrjNumR]
			@CompanyId = @CompanyId,
			@prjNumR = @prjNumR,
			@CurrId = @ProjectId,
			@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
			@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

		IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
		BEGIN
			SET @IsDuplicate = 1
		END
	END
	ELSE IF(@CantSaveDupPrjNumRByAllPrj = 1)
	BEGIN
		EXEC [MobspCheckDuplicatePrjNumR]
			@CompanyId = 0,
			@prjNumR = @prjNumR,
			@CurrId = @ProjectId,
			@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
			@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

		IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
		BEGIN
			SET @IsDuplicate = 1
		END
	END
END
/******************************/ /*AI14020718 - Add check*/
IF(ISNULL(@prjValidityPrdId, 0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@prjValidityOrdItemId, 0) <> 0)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @SelectedValidity INT = NULL
	EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckProjectValidityOrdItem]
		@OrderItemId = @prjValidityOrdItemId,
		@CurrentDate = @RegisterDate,
		@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
		@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
		@SelectedValidity = @SelectedValidity OUTPUT

	IF(ISNULL(@SelectedValidity, 0) = 0)
	BEGIN
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'Msg=8'
	END
END
/******************************/
/*AI14000201 End*/
/*************************************************************/
END
ELSE
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=3'

/**/
/******************************* CHECK Access comapny and datapermission *************************/
/*RZ971222- Add check access to cmp and datapermission*/
/**/
IF(@CompanyId IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @IsAccessCompanyId BIT = 0

	EXEC dbo.MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 	
	@Mode = 8,
	@Id = @CompanyId,
	@CrmUserId = @UserId,
	@IsAccess = @IsAccessCompanyId OUTPUT
				
	IF(@IsAccessCompanyId = 0)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @FoundCmpName NVARCHAR(2000)
		SET @FoundCmpName = (SELECT
							CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
								THEN (SELECT  
										ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId),'') + ' ' +
										ISNULL(cmpName,'')
								FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @CompanyId)
								ELSE (SELECT  
										ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' +
										ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CL WHERE CL.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CL.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
										FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @CompanyId)
							END
							)
									
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=CompanyAccess|' + CAST(@ProjectId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '|PRJID|' + ISNULL(@FoundCmpName,'') + '|Msg=CompanyAccess|'
	END	 
END
/**/
IF(@ProjectId IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @prjIdFound INT 
										
	SET @prjIdFound = (SELECT ProjectID 
						FROM dbo.CrmProjects P
						WHERE ProjectID = @ProjectId		
						AND prjTitle NOT IN 
						(SELECT ForbiddenProjectTitle 
						FROM CrmProjectsForbiddenForUser
						WHERE CrmUserId = @UserID))
						
	IF(@prjIdFound IS NULL)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @FoundPrjTitle NVARCHAR(2000)
		SET @FoundPrjTitle = (SELECT
							CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
								THEN (SELECT prjTitle
								FROM dbo.CrmProjects P WHERE P.ProjectID = @ProjectId)
								ELSE (SELECT prjTitle
										FROM dbo.CrmProjects_Lang P WHERE P.ProjectID = @ProjectId AND P.LanguageID = @LanguageID)
							END
							)

		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=PrjAccess|' + CAST(@ProjectId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '|PRJID|' + ISNULL(@FoundPrjTitle,'') + '|Msg=PrjAccess|'
	END
END

/*RZ971222- END*/

/*==============================================================================*/
/****************************** Check for change status group *******************/
DECLARE @FindPrjCnvsStatusGroupId INT, @CountConversation INT = 0 
SET @FindPrjCnvsStatusGroupId = (SELECT TOP 1 P.prjcnvsStatusGroupId FROM dbo.CrmProjects P WHERE P.ProjectID = @ProjectId) 
IF(@FindPrjCnvsStatusGroupId IS NOT NULL AND  @FindPrjCnvsStatusGroupId <> @prjcnvsStatusGroupId)
BEGIN
	EXEC dbo.MobspCheckUsedPrjCNVSStatusGroup  
	@CompanyId = @CompanyId,
	@ProjectId = @ProjectId,
	@prjcnvsStatusGroupId = @FindPrjCnvsStatusGroupId,
	@CountConversation = @CountConversation OUTPUT	
END

/**/
/*AI14000201*/
IF(@IsDuplicate = 1)/* AND @TypeTable <> 'Dup')*/ /* @IsDuplicate = 1*/
BEGIN
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'Dup=1Msg=0'	                           
END
IF(@ErrorMessage = '' OR @ErrorMessage IS NULL)
BEGIN
IF(@CountConversation = 0)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @FindCompanyId INT
	SET @FindCompanyId = (SELECT TOP 1 CompanyID FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @CompanyId)
	IF(@FindCompanyId IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		UPDATE [dbo].[CrmProjects]
		   SET 
			   [prjTitle] = @prjTitle
			  ,[prjDesc] = @prjDesc
			  ,[prjStartDate] = @prjStartDate

			  ,[RegisterDatePersian] = @RegisterDatePersian		  
			  ,[prjGroupId] = @prjGroupId
			  ,[prjcnvsStatusGroupId] = @prjcnvsStatusGroupId
			  ,[prjOrderView] = @prjOrderView
			  ,[AppType] = @AppType
			  ,[prjPostFix] = @prjPostFix		  
			  ,[prjRemindDate] = @prjRemindDate
			  ,[prjRemindDateTime] = @prjRemindDateTime

			  ,[prjHaveSms] = @prjHaveSms

			  ,[prjSmsText] = @prjSmsText
			  ,[prjSmsSendDate] = @prjSmsSendDate
			  ,[prjSmsSendDatePersian] = @prjSmsSendDatePersian

			  ,[prjSubject] = @prjSubject

			  ,[prjCategoryId] = @prjCategoryId
			  ,[prjDescI] = @prjDescI
			  ,[prjNumRef] = @prjNumRef
			  ,[prjNumR] = @prjNumR
			  /*AI14000131 End*/
			  /*AI14010317*/
			  ,[prjToDoTime] = @prjToDoDateTime
			  ,[prjToDoTimeRegister] = @prjToDoRegisterDateTime
			  ,[prjToDoTimeDone] = @prjToDoDoneDateTime
			  ,[prjFOCmpCanInsCnvs] = @prjFOCmpCanInsCnvs
			  ,[prjFOCmpCanNotInsCnvs] = @prjFOCmpCanNotInsCnvs
			  ,[prjTicketStop] = @prjTicketStop
			  ,[prjIsTicketNew] = @prjIsTicketNew
			  /*AI14010317-End*/
			  ,[prjToDoTimeSec] = @prjToDoTimeSec /*AI14010412*/
			  ,[prjToDoTimeRegisterHistory] = @prjToDoTimeRegisterHistory /*AI14010416*/
			  /*AI14010728*/
			  ,[prjcmpPresentationId] = @prjcmpPresentationId
			  ,[prjcmpCampaignIds] = @prjcmpCampaignIds
			  /*AI14010728-End*/
			  ,[prjcmpQuickRegisterDefSettingId] = @prjcmpQuickRegisterDefSettingId /*AI14020504*/
			  /*AI14020718*/
			  ,[prjValidityPrdId] = @prjValidityPrdId
			  ,[prjValidityOrdItemId] = @prjValidityOrdItemId
			  /*AI14020718-End*/
			WHERE ProjectID = @ProjectId
		/******* Update to lang table ********/
		UPDATE [dbo].[CrmProjects_Lang]
		   SET 
			   [prjTitle] = @prjTitle
			  ,[prjDesc] = @prjDesc
			  ,[prjPostFix] = @prjPostFix
		 WHERE ProjectID = @ProjectId
		 AND [LanguageID] IN (SELECT LN.LanguageID FROM dbo.Languages LN 
								WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
								AND LN.IsActive = 1)
		/*********** Insert to Forbidden *********************/
		IF(@prjInsertForbidden = 1)
		BEGIN
			EXEC dbo.MobspInsertCrmProjectsForbiddenForUserFromDefineProject 
			@CrmUserId = @UserId,
			@ForbiddenProjectTitle = @prjTitle,
			@FirstRegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
			@FirstRegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
			@FirstRegisterUserId  = @UserId,
			@UserId  = @UserId,
			@Year1  = @CurrYear,
			@Month1 = @Month1,
			@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
			@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
			@AppType = @AppType,
			@LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID  
		END	
	
		/*AI14010407*/
		IF(ISNULL(@prjIsTicket, 0) = 1 AND ISNULL(@prjIsFinished, 0) = 1)
		BEGIN
			EXEC [dbo].[MobspCloseTicket]
				@ProjectId = @ProjectId,
				@UserId  = @UserId,
				@Year1  = @CurrYear,
				@Month1 = @Month1,
				@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
				@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
				@AppType = @AppType
		END
		/*AI14010407-End*/
		/*AI14010408*/
		DECLARE @CnvsIsTicket BIT = NULL
		IF(ISNULL(@prjIsTicket, 0) = 1)
		BEGIN
			SET @CnvsIsTicket = 1
		END

		EXEC [dbo].[MobspUpdateConversationIsTicketByPrjId]
			@ProjectId = @ProjectId,
			@cnvsIsTicket = @CnvsIsTicket,
			@UserId  = @UserId,
			@Year1  = @CurrYear,
			@Month1 = @Month1,
			@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
			@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
			@AppType = @AppType
		/*AI14010408-End*/
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage  + '|Msg=0'
	END	
END
ELSE
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage  + '|Msg=4'
END
/**/
IF (@@ERROR > 0)
BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Update Project' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
ELSE 
COMMIT TRAN
/**/
/**/
SET @UpdatedProjectId = @ProjectId
/**/
IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
BEGIN 
	RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
 	SELECT 0
END 
ELSE
BEGIN
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	SELECT 1
END	
/**/
END

    BEGIN
        SET @CnvsField = SUBSTRING(@Conversation,
                                    0,

		/*AI14020429*/
		/*--------------------------*/
		/*END CONVERSATION*/						
        /*END GENERAL*/
        /*---------------------------- END Fill variabele --------------------------------*/
        /*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/
        SET @Conversation = SUBSTRING(@Conversation,
                                  LEN(@CnvsField + '|#|') + 1,
                                  LEN(@Conversation))
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SET @CnvsField = @Conversation
        SET @Conversation = NULL        
    END
END

/******************************* CHECK Access comapny and datapermission *************************/
/*RZ971222- Add check access to cmp and datapermission*/
/**/
IF(@CompanyId IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @IsAccessCompanyId BIT = 0

	EXEC dbo.MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 	
	@Mode = 8,
	@Id = @CompanyId,
	@CrmUserId = @UserId,
	@IsAccess = @IsAccessCompanyId OUTPUT
				
	IF(@IsAccessCompanyId = 0)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @FoundCmpName NVARCHAR(2000)
		SET @FoundCmpName = (SELECT
							CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
								THEN (SELECT  
										ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId),'') + ' ' +
										ISNULL(cmpName,'')
								FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @CompanyId)
								ELSE (SELECT  
										ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' +
										ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CL WHERE CL.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CL.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
										FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @CompanyId)
							END
							)
									
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=CompanyAccess|' + CAST(@ConversationId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '|CNVSID|' + ISNULL(@FoundCmpName,'') + '|Msg=CompanyAccess|'
	END	 
END
/**/
IF(@ProjectId IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @prjIdFound INT 
										
	SET @prjIdFound = (SELECT ProjectID 
						FROM dbo.CrmProjects P
						WHERE ProjectID = @ProjectId		
						AND prjTitle NOT IN 
						(SELECT ForbiddenProjectTitle 
						FROM CrmProjectsForbiddenForUser
						WHERE CrmUserId = @UserID))
						
	IF(@prjIdFound IS NULL)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @FoundPrjTitle NVARCHAR(2000)
		SET @FoundPrjTitle = (SELECT
							CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
								THEN (SELECT prjTitle
								FROM dbo.CrmProjects P WHERE P.ProjectID = @ProjectId)
								ELSE (SELECT prjTitle
										FROM dbo.CrmProjects_Lang P WHERE P.ProjectID = @ProjectId AND P.LanguageID = @LanguageID)
							END
							)

		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=PrjAccess|' + CAST(@ConversationId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '|CNVSID|' + ISNULL(@FoundPrjTitle,'') + '|Msg=PrjAccess|'
	END
END

	@Mode = 2
	,@ConversationID = @ConversationId

	,@cnvsProjectId = @ProjectId
	,@cnvsPersonId = @cnvsPersonId
	,@cnvsStatusId = @cnvsStatusId

	,@cnvsDoingTimeSec = @cnvsDoingTimeSec
	,@CurrentUserId = @UserId
	,@LanguageID = @LanguageID
	,@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

	,@ErrorMessage = @PreChecksErrorMesssage OUTPUT
